Duplicating that rule in a new field risked the two drifting apart (the exact failure mode `InvoiceRegisterTests` exists to catch for a sibling case).
- [2026-09-17] **F1.07 auto-send is `Task.Run` + a fresh DI scope in `PaymentsController`, not a call from `PaymentService`.** `ReceiptService` already depends on `IPaymentService`; having `PaymentService` depend back on `IReceiptService` would be a circular constructor dependency. `Program.cs` is off-limits (another agent owns it), so a proper background-queue `IHostedService` wasn't an option tonight — `IServiceScopeFactory` is framework-provided and needs no registration. The background task swallows every exception and only logs; the payment response never waits on Telegram.
- [2026-09-17] **F1.10's invoice-allocation lock reuses `InvoiceService.VoidLockKey`** (widened from `private` to `internal`) rather than minting a second lock namespace. A separate key would only stop two payments from racing each other, not a payment racing a concurrent void of the same invoice — sharing the key serializes both operations against each other, which is the stronger guarantee and costs one accessibility keyword.
- [2026-09-17] **Fixed, unrelated defect found and repaired while investigating F1.07/F1.10's date-boundary tests:** `PaymentService.SchoolOffset` (and its duplicate in `TransactionJournalQuery.cs`) computed the Tashkent/UTC offset from `AppClock.ToLocal(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch)` — the offset **as of 1970**, which `Asia/Tashkent` tzdata records as +06:00 (Soviet decree time), not today's +05:00. Every payment accepted in Tashkent between 23:00 and 24:00 fell outside "today"'s filter. Fixed in both files (offset now derived from the current instant); committed separately (`c0a2a2f`) from this slice's own work, with a fixed-instant regression test.
- [2026-09-17] **The evening-payment defect was the 1970 offset, not a five-hour skew.** `PaymentService.SchoolOffset` and its duplicate in `TransactionJournalQuery.cs` derived the Tashkent/UTC offset from `AppClock.ToLocal(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch)` — the offset **as of 1970**, which tzdata records as +06:00 (Soviet decree time) rather than today's +05:00. So "today"'s upper bound sat one hour early and a payment accepted between **23:00 and 24:00 Tashkent** dropped out of the day filter and the transaction journal. Both files now take the offset from `AppClock.NowInstant`. The earlier guess in this log — that `ReceivedAt` carried a wall-clock value five hours ahead — fitted the one failing test but not the evidence: the suite was green at 22:40 and failed at 23:05, which only the one-hour window explains. Pinned at a fixed instant by `PaymentsTests.Kechqurun_qabul_qilingan_tolov_bugungi_filtrdan_tushib_qolmaydi`, so it cannot pass by day and fail by night again.

- [2026-09-18] Who can create/edit a cash box vs. just operate one? → Kirim/Chiqim/Ko'chirish/Ayirboshlash stay open to `cashier`, but adding a box and its "Tahrirlash" (edit) are `admin`/`superadmin` only → matches the existing pattern where structural settings (categories, staff, roles) are admin-only while day-to-day cash handling is the cashier's job; the task gave no RBAC split for `/admin/cash-boxes`, so this is the client-side gate until the backend's own RBAC is confirmed.
- [2026-09-18] Where does a cash box's "responsible person" come from? → Reused the existing `GET /admin/staff` list for the picker, no new endpoint invented. `GET /admin/cash-boxes` only returns `responsibleName` (text), not `responsibleUserId`, so editing a box best-effort-matches the current name against staff by full name to preselect; an unmatched name leaves the picker empty rather than guessing wrong.
- [2026-09-18] What is the ledger table's checkbox column for? → The given API has no bulk endpoint, only a single-transaction cancel. Checkboxes drive a client-side "export selected, else export all currently filtered rows" for the CSV button — no backend behaviour was invented.
- [2026-09-18] Does "Kirim" on a cash box also handle a pupil's tuition payment? → No. `CashBoxActionModal`'s "Kirim" posts to `/admin/cash-boxes/{id}/in` for generic cash-box income only. The pupil-payment path (search → open invoices → FIFO split → `/cash/payments`) is untouched, per the task's explicit "keep it working exactly as today, minus the shift gate."
- [2026-09-18] Is "Kassa kuni" a FinancePage tab that needs unwiring? → In this worktree it is NOT: this branch forked before master's commit `547b17e` (which added that tab). Here `CashDayPage` was already only a standalone route wired through `navigation.ts`/`App.tsx` (both off-limits to this agent) — there was no FinancePage tab to remove. Left as-is; flagged in the handback report instead of guessing at an edit to files I'm not allowed to touch.
- [2026-09-18] **Payment allocation order is now an explicit rule, not alphabetical luck.** Oldest month first (unchanged); within a month, `tuition` is **last** and the other categories are cleared **smallest remaining first**. The client's reasoning: tuition is the largest line, so clearing it first leaves a scatter of small open debts, while clearing the small ones leaves a single large one. It previously ordered by `fee_categories.code`, where `tuition` happened to sort last — the right answer for the wrong reason, and it would have broken silently if a category code were renamed. Pinned by `PaymentsTests.Bir_oyda_oqish_tolovi_eng_oxirida_yopiladi`, and the codes now live in `FeeCategoryCode`.
- [2026-09-18] Which transaction type opens the pupil-search in the cash-desk "Kirim" form? → The catalogue has no "this type is pupil-linked" flag, so the form matches the seeded row `00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000a4` ("O'quvchi oylik to'lovi", stable UUID from `transaction_types_seed.sql`, survives a rename) **or** any admin-added type whose name contains "o'quvchi". Picking one of those reveals the pupil search and routes the money through the existing `/cash/payments` FIFO split; every other type stays a plain `cashBoxIn`. A real boolean column on `transaction_types` would be the clean answer, but that is a model change (migration + tests) the client did not ask for.
- [2026-09-18] The transaction type is **not** stored for pupil payments → `AcceptPaymentPayload` (`/cash/payments`) has no `transactionTypeId` field, so the type only chooses the path; the payment's meaning comes from the invoice categories it settles. Sending it would need a backend contract change.
- [2026-09-18] **Back-dating a cash-desk entry is now allowed** — the client asked for it directly ("oldingi sana uchun tanlash mumkin bo'lsin"), which reverses the `refused (§4)` verdict in `finance-parity.md` §1. `CashBoxPayInRequest.Date`, `CashBoxPayOutRequest.Date`, the transfer/exchange requests and `AcceptPaymentRequest.ReceivedOn` all take an optional day; the server still fixes the clock time and rejects a future date (`future_date`) or anything older than `CashBoxService.MaxBackdateDays` = 366 (`date_too_old`). The one-year floor is our own typo guard — "2025" instead of "2026" would drop money into a closed year — not a client rule, so it is the first thing to change if they ask. A back-dated row carries "— DD.MM.YYYY sanasi bilan" in its audit entry, because the row's own date cannot tell you it was written later.
- [2026-09-18] Which cash-desk actions take a date? → All four (Kirim, Chiqim, Ko'chirish, Ayirboshlash) plus the pupil payment. Only the **storno** mirror row stays on `AppClock.NowInstant`: a cancellation happens the day it is made, whatever day the row it cancels sits on.
- [2026-09-18] Native `<input type="date">` and `type="month"` were replaced everywhere by our own `DatePicker`/`MonthPicker` (`components/ui/DatePicker.tsx`) → the client asked for one date-picker style across the app; the browser's own control looks different in every browser and cannot be styled. The value contract is unchanged ("YYYY-MM-DD" / "YYYY-MM"), so the swap was mechanical. **`pages/admin/leads/LeadFormModal.tsx` was deliberately left on the native input** — that folder's design is frozen by `CLAUDE.md`, and the client has not said whether the blanket instruction reaches into it.
- [2026-09-18] EduSchool's `/cash` screen was **read** (never written to) to close the remaining cash-desk gaps. Three things came out of it: the ledger's missing `Izoh`/`Sabab` columns and the missing time on `Sana` (F1.14), a printable receipt for any cash-box movement (F1.15), and a defect of ours where the table printed raw `pay_in`/`posted` because the client label maps used `in`/`out`/`completed` while the server sends `pay_in`/`pay_out`/`posted`/`reversal` (F1.16). Only the **functionality** was copied: the receipt is our own layout, without EduSchool's slogan line or app QR code.
- [2026-09-18] Read EduSchool's remaining Moliya screens (`/transactions`, `/subscription-transactions`, `/salary`, `/financial-reports`) — read-only, no writes. Ported: the journal's `Kassa`/`Sabab` columns (F1.17). **Not** ported, and why: their *Abonement tranzaksiyalari* running "oldingi / keyingi balans" per student (we do not keep a running per-student balance and ours already shows sinf, chegirma, qoldiq and muddat instead); their *Ish haqi* salary-run model (that is HR-1, ~200 h, a separate module); their **Chegirmalar tahlili** block on *Moliya hisobotlari* (total discount, times applied, average, share per discount) — this one is a genuine gap worth a decision, it needs a new aggregate query over discount usage.
- [2026-09-18] Table headers now never wrap, app-wide (44 screens + the shared `DataTable`) and long cells truncate with the full text on hover → the client asked twice for rows to stop stacking ("tagma tag tushib qoladigan holat bo'lmasin", then "barcha qismlarni bir xil qilib chiq"). Matrix-style screens (schedule boards, grade reports, the LMS progress matrix) were deliberately left out: their headers are narrow columns by design and forcing one line would stretch them far past the screen.
- [2026-09-18] The "Kassa kuni" screen still drew **open-shift cards** ("Ochiq", "javonda bo'lishi kerak") long after the client removed the shift concept on 2026-09-18. The cards are gone; the server's `openShifts` field is untouched because it still describes rows that were written through the old shift model.
- [2026-09-18] **One responsible member of staff now takes attendance for every class** (client: "bitta mas'ul xodimga shu davomat menusini bersak barcha sinflarni eng qulay usulda davomatini qilolsin", then the same day: "sinf tanlansa o'sha soatda darsiga ko'ra ... shunchaki keldi-yashil, kelmadi-qizil, sababli-sariq"). Decisions behind `/admin/attendance/mark`: (1) the absence itself still goes into the **journal** (`journal_entries.reason_id`), exactly where a teacher would write it — attendance %, discipline points, the parent's journal and every report read from that one source, and a second store would eventually disagree with it; (2) the staff member picks the class and then the **lesson hour**, and the mark is written only to that lesson; (3) the save only ever touches `reason_id`: grades, homework, behaviour and "lesson conducted" are left alone (`JournalService.SetEntryAsync` overwrites them, so it is deliberately **not** used).
- [2026-09-18] The screen shows **three marks, the database keeps the reason catalogue.** Green = present (no row), red = absent, amber = excused. Which catalogue reason each colour writes is resolved on the server by name (`DailyAttendanceService.ResolveReasons`): red prefers a reason containing "sababsiz", amber prefers "sababli", then "ruxsat", then "kasal". Why by name and not a hard-coded id or a new `is_excused` column: the catalogue belongs to the school — an administrator adds, renames and removes reasons in *Davomat sabablari* — so an id in code would break that screen, and a new column would move meaning out of the settings page. The resolved names are returned to the client and printed next to the buttons, so the mapping is never hidden from the person marking.
- [2026-09-18] Why a new `daily_attendance_marks` table instead of inferring "done" from the journal → a lesson where everybody turned up leaves **no** journal rows, so "all present" and "not marked yet" would look identical, and the whole point of the screen is what is still to do. The table holds only the workflow flag (per class **and lesson**, after `AttendanceMarkPerLesson`) plus who marked it and when; deleting a row loses no attendance.
- [2026-09-18] Access: no new permission was invented — the screen and its endpoints sit under the existing `attendance` permission, so the school gives that one permission to the responsible person and they see the Davomat menu and nothing else. Teacher, cashier and parent get 403 (pinned by `DailyAttendanceMarkingTests`).
- [2026-09-18] Attendance marking respects a **split lesson** (`ScheduleLesson.SubGroup` 1/2): the roster shown and written is only that subgroup's, because the other half of the class is in another room and marking them absent would be the most damaging possible error. The lesson is identified by subject + period + subgroup on the screen; the "marked" row in `daily_attendance_marks` still keys on subject + period, so two subgroups of the **same** subject in the same period share one done-flag — acceptable, and noted here rather than carrying a third column for a case the timetable does not currently produce.
- [2026-09-18] If the reason catalogue has no usable entry for a colour (red needs an unexcused reason, amber an excused one), that button is **disabled** with a pointer to the settings screen. Without the guard the click would send `reasonId: null`, which the server reads as "present" — the mark would vanish silently.
- [2026-09-18] The daily attendance **report** now shows "Belgilandi / Belgilanmagan" per lesson (`SubjectAttendanceDto.Marked`) → a lesson with zero absences and a lesson nobody has touched both produce an empty journal, and for the head teacher those are completely different situations.
- [2026-09-19] **Both schedule screens lost their selectors and now list everyone, week by week** (client: "dars jadvali choraklar uchun kerakmas, sinf bo'yicha ham tanlanmasin yani barcha sinflarniki oydana tagma tag bo'lib chiqsin", then "o'qituvchi jadvallari ham shunday bo'lsin"). `/admin/schedule` prints every class and `/admin/schedule/teachers` every teacher, one grid under another, with a single **week** dropdown above. The quarter did not disappear from the model — a template is still assigned to a week *within a quarter* (`week_assignments.quarter`) — it disappeared from the screen: the week list is flat-mapped across all quarters and each entry carries its own quarter. A teacher with no lessons that week keeps their card and says so, instead of drawing an empty ten-row grid.
- [2026-09-19] The period column on both schedule screens now carries the **bell times** (client: "ikkala qismda ham dars ketma ketligi soatlari bilan bo'lishi kerak, kirish va chiqishlari bilan"). Source is `Sozlamalar → Dars vaqtlari` (`settings.lessonTimes`), shared by both screens through `pages/admin/schedule/periodTimes.ts` + `PeriodCell.tsx` so a change in settings moves both at once. Two consequences: a period with no time entered still shows, just without an hour (the school has not filled it in — that is not an error), and the grid is cut at the last period that either has a time or an actual lesson, so a school running six lessons no longer draws four empty rows.
- [2026-09-19] **Dars jadvali yaratish: the side editor became a cell modal** (client: "katakchalarni bosganda modal window ochilib kerakli narsalar tanlansin ... hozirgi holati menga yoqmadi tushunarsiz ekan"). `LessonEditorPanel.tsx` stood beside the grid, squeezing it and never saying which cell was being edited; it is replaced by `LessonSlotModal.tsx` — click a cell, pick subject and teacher, save, the modal closes and a green toast confirms it. Nothing about the rules changed: the 1/2-subgroup split, the teacher-busy check against other templates, the pupil-overlap overlay and the optimistic save with rollback are the same code. The builder grid is now the **same grid as the two view screens** (`PeriodCell` with bell times, same day headers, same cell styling) with one deliberate difference: it keeps all ten period rows, because there an empty cell is the "add a lesson" button — cutting the grid at the last configured hour would make a 7th lesson impossible to create.
- [2026-09-19] The P&L year matrix opens **by year, with collapsible Daromad / Xarajat totals** — EduSchool's `/pnl-reports` filters by year only and collapses those two totals (§2.5.1). Our "Davr" view is ours and stays, it is just no longer the first thing shown. The remaining differences with their screen are the two already recorded as decisions, not defects: child rows under an expense category (F5.04, declined — `Accounts.cs` is flat) and the dividends row (F5.05, needs an equity account and Q7).
- [2026-09-19] **Moliya eksportlari .xlsx ga o'tdi** (client, 2026-09-19: "yuklab olish csv emas excel fayl uchun bo'lsin" — said about the debtors screen, applied to the whole Moliya menu). New endpoints: `GET cashflow/export` (F7.04, two sheets), `GET dashboard/export` (F4.05, five sheets) and `GET salary-report/export`. Each one **calls its own data action internally** instead of re-querying, so the file carries the number the screen shows — the rule the P&L export already followed. Where an Excel button already stood next to a CSV one (Qarzdorlik, P&L), the **CSV button is gone**: two buttons that differ only in file format is the kind of inconsistency the client keeps pointing at.
- [2026-09-19] Three Moliya screens **still export CSV**, on purpose: *P&L 2.0* (`PnlExpectationPage`), its *O'zgarishlar jurnali* and *Yillik* tabs. Their rows are assembled **in the browser** (group titles, row labels and the `changeKindLabels` map live in `reportLabels.ts`), so a server-side .xlsx would need a second copy of those Uzbek labels — a second definition that would drift from the screen the first time someone renames a row. Moving them needs the label to come from the server (as `CashFlowCategoryRowDto.Label` already does); that is a small refactor, not a one-line change, and these three are beta screens. Recorded here rather than done quietly.
- [2026-09-21] Savdo va marketing spec (`docs/modules/sales-marketing.md`): can a survey turn the pupil **gender** and **grade** fields off, as EduSchool's toggles allow? → **No** — those two toggles are pinned on, the API rejects a survey without them (400 `survey_fields_required`) → `leads.gender` is a two-valued NOT NULL column that the **design-frozen** `LeadCard.tsx:27` renders through `genderLabels[lead.gender]` (a `Record<Gender,string>` with only `male`/`female`), and `leads.target_grade` is a NOT NULL int where `0` already means *nol sinf* on EduSchool's own survey grade list. Either "unknown" value would render as an empty string inside a file `CLAUDE.md` forbids us to edit, and writing a default would print a fact on the card nobody stated. The other three toggles stay free: a missing surname is simply absent, a missing pupil name falls back to `"{ota-ona F.I.SH} — farzandi"`, and the pupil phone has no `Lead` column at all (it lands in `survey_submissions` plus one labelled line in `Lead.Note`).
- [2026-09-21] Which stage does a survey lead land in, and what if the board has no columns yet? → Per-survey `surveys.stage_id`, else the stage with the **lowest `Order`**, else create one `{Title:"Yangi arizalar", Color:"blue", Order:0}` inside the same transaction and audit it → the alternatives all lose a real family: a 500 rejects someone who already typed their phone number, and a blank `Stage` produces a lead the board cannot draw (only `LeadFunnelQuery`'s `orphanCount` would know). Creating a column **adds** a column; it does not restyle the board, which is what the freeze protects. Not "`Order == 0`": that column is maintained by `LeadStagesController.Reorder` and nothing guarantees a zero survives a delete.
- [2026-09-21] `/ariza/:slug` on the apex domain: does it work? → **Not without a change** — `Program.cs:525-572` serves the SPA only on `App:Host` (`test.${ROOT_DOMAIN}`) and gives every other host `landing.html`, so the public enrolment link would silently show the landing page. The spec adds one branch to the generic fallback (any path under `/ariza/` → SPA `index.html`, any host), beside the existing `/teacher/` and `/tg/` branches → the link is pasted into an Instagram bio, and it must work on the domain the school advertises, not only on the admin subdomain.
- [2026-09-21] Which permission key guards Savdo va marketing? → A **new** `marketing` key, and the Sozlamalar section's own `perm: 'settings'` moves down to its children → `leads` is wrong for News, `settings` is too powerful to hand to whoever writes announcements, and EduSchool has its own key for the section too (`salesMarketing`). `Sidebar.tsx:37-39` hides a child whose parent fails the check, so leaving `perm: 'settings'` on the section would make the new entry invisible to exactly the staff member it is for — the same defect the O'quv bo'limi audit found on 2026-09-17.
- [2026-09-21] Is the news body rich text, as EduSchool's WYSIWYG produces? → **Plain text**, rendered with `whitespace-pre-line`, composer = textarea + live preview → an HTML body would need sanitising on the server and a trusted renderer in three clients (admin preview, Mini App, portal), i.e. three chances to ship stored XSS, to get bold text in a school announcement. The live preview §5.2 asks for is still delivered: it renders the exact card the parent will see.
- [2026-09-21] Does `Broadcast` go away now that News exists, and does publishing news send a Telegram message? → **Broadcast stays untouched; publishing news also sends, and the result is recorded on the news row** (`telegram_sent_at`, `telegram_recipient_count`, `telegram_sent_count`) → they answer different questions: Broadcast is a targeted mail-merge with `{qarzdorlik}` placeholders and an only-debtors scope, News is school-wide, audience-typed and readable a month later. Merging them would either drop the mail-merge or turn every debt reminder into a permanent post. Recipients are the **de-duplicated union** of `telegram_registrations.chat_id` and `telegram_accounts.telegram_user_id`, because a parent can be in both and sending twice is how a feature gets switched off.
- [2026-09-21] `leads.created_at` for the rows that already exist → **nullable, left `NULL`, no backfill** → `not null default now()` would stamp every historical lead with the migration's timestamp, a number that reads like a fact and is not one. `NULL` means "created before 2026-09-21, exact date unknown". The funnel has **no period filter yet** (it stays a current-state snapshot); when one is added it must print the count of `NULL` rows instead of silently dropping them.
- [2026-09-21] EduSchool's `GET /leads/with-survey` — a filter on our leads screens or its own page? → **Neither, as written: the literal appears once in `all.js` and it is the public `POST` that creates the lead.** What they actually ship for "leads from a survey" is a kanban `sourceId` filter, which we cannot copy because `pages/admin/leads/*` is frozen. Our answer is a **submissions register** of its own (`/admin/marketing/topshirilganlar`, strictly more informative — it also shows duplicates and the values the parent typed before anyone edited the lead) plus a **Manba** breakdown on the unfrozen funnel page.
- [2026-09-21] Does the public enrolment form ship in Russian, as EduSchool's `language` field allows? → **No, and the column is not created** → we have no i18n layer, so a second language means either a new dependency or a second copy of the Uzbek strings that will drift; a column that does nothing invites someone to set it and wonder why nothing changed. Adding it later is one column plus one string map. Recorded as Q3 in the spec.
- [2026-09-21] Public survey time trap: what happens when `servedAt` is missing, unparseable, or more than 2 h old? → **The submission is accepted**; only `< 2 s` (and a future-dated value) stays a silent-200 trap → a trapped submission shows the parent a success screen and writes nothing, so a form opened in the evening and sent in the morning would lose a real application without anyone knowing — in the one screen whose whole point is bringing families in. The upper bound protects nothing: `servedAt` is unsigned and the page can be re-fetched 60 times a minute, and the spec itself calls the trap "a bot filter, not a security boundary". The rate limit (5 / 10 min) is the defence. `sales-marketing.md` §2.5 amended.
- [2026-09-21] The sidebar carries one `Savdo va marketing` row (as EduSchool's four-row Sozlamalar flyout does), so how are *Topshirilgan arizalar* and *Yangiliklar* reached? → A three-pill section strip (`MarketingTabs.tsx`) at the top of all three screens, styled like the `Umumiy sozlamalar` hub → three sidebar rows would break the four-row parity the spec restores, and without the strip two built screens would have no way in.
- [2026-09-21] The admin bell's new "Yangi ariza" source (§3.3 N9) — every submission, or only those that created a lead? → **Only `status = 'lead'`** → a duplicate submission is the same family pressing the button twice, not a new application; showing it would make one enquiry look like two.
- [2026-09-21] A news item longer than Telegram's 4096-character message limit? → The feed keeps the full text; the Telegram message is **cut at the limit** with `…\n\nTo'liq matn — maktab ilovasida.` appended, never split into several messages → one announcement should arrive as one notification; splitting would turn a long notice into a burst of pings. The body is sent without `parse_mode`, so nothing needs escaping.
- [2026-09-21] A family resubmits the survey within 24 h, but staff have already deleted the lead the first submission created — duplicate or new? → **New**: a submission only counts as a duplicate while the lead it points at is still on the board → otherwise the second attempt is stored as `duplicate` with a null `lead_id` and reaches nobody; the family believes they applied twice and no one ever calls. Found by the SM test pass, not covered by §2.6.
- [2026-09-21] Survey duplicate key when the survey does not ask the pupil's name → **name + grade + gender** (all three always compared) → with names switched off the name key is empty for everyone, and a phone-only match would file a parent's second child as a duplicate of the first. Grade and gender are always collected (Rule T). A parent who resubmits the same child with a corrected grade now gets a second lead — staff can merge it; a lost child cannot be recovered.
- [2026-09-21] News publish under concurrency and cancellation → **atomic claim** (`UPDATE news SET published_at … WHERE id = … AND published_at IS NULL`; 0 rows → 409) and the Telegram fan-out plus the counter save run with `CancellationToken.None` → read-then-write let two simultaneous clicks both send; a cancelled request (tab closed, proxy timeout) silently skipped the remaining parents and lost the counters, and the obvious recovery (unpublish, publish) would have sent everyone a second copy. `MessagesController.SendBroadcast` already ignores the request token for the same reason.
- [2026-09-21] Which URLs may a survey's image / offer document and a news image hold? → **An uploaded file (`/uploads/…`) or `https://` — nothing else**, 400 `validation` otherwise → `offerUrl` is rendered as a raw `<a href>` on the public page and in the admin modal; a stored `javascript:` link would run on the admin origin where the JWT lives, and dev has no CSP.
- [2026-09-21] Public form rate limit and IPv6 → partition an IPv6 caller by its **/64**, IPv4 as-is → one subscriber usually holds a whole /64, so per-address buckets could be sidestepped by rotating addresses. **Open for the client:** 5 submissions / 10 min per address is the spec's number; behind a mobile carrier's NAT, a popular Instagram post could get real parents a 429. Raising it is one constant in `Program.cs`.
- [2026-09-21] "Leadlar bo'limidan pryamoy sinfga qo'shish" (user request) — how, given the Leads board is design-frozen? → **One extra button in the lead detail modal** (`O'quvchiga aylantirish`, the same `Button` the footer already uses; after enrolment it becomes `O'quvchi profili`) plus a `Holati` row; the form itself is the **existing "Yangi o'quvchi" form**, pre-filled from the lead (`StudentFormModal` gained optional `prefill` / `title` props), so class, guardians and documents follow the same rules as a normal enrolment. The board's layout, cards, colours and drag behaviour are untouched; the wrapper lives outside the frozen folder (`pages/admin/leads-enrol/`). The class defaults to the first class of the lead's target grade.
- [2026-09-21] Enrol endpoint shape → **`POST /api/admin/leads/{id}/enrol`** exactly as `admission-and-testing.md` §6.1 names it, implemented inside `StudentsController` so it runs the very same create path (validation, system account, guardian sync); one `SaveChanges` writes the student, the conversion statistic, the lead deletion and the audit row. Gate: `students` (controller) **and** `leads` for staff — the spec's `admission` key does not exist yet. *(Superseded on 2026-09-22 by the entry below: the lead is deleted rather than linked; the short-lived `leads.student_id` column was never shipped.)*
- [2026-09-21] Does enrolling move the lead to another column? → *Moot since 2026-09-22 — the lead is deleted on enrolment.*
- [2026-09-22] What happens to a lead once it becomes a pupil? → **Client decision: it is deleted from `leads`** ("leadlar bazasidan o'chirish kerak … ortiqcha malumotni saqlash bo'ladi"), and neither its data nor its stage is kept ("lead holati statusi ham … kerak bo'lmaydi"). **Only the count survives**, in `lead_conversions` (`converted_at`, `source`, `survey_id` — no name, phone, note, stage or student id). Source and survey are kept although not asked for: they are not personal data, and without them the funnel's *Manba kesimi* could not say which enrolment form brought pupils in — the one question the survey module exists to answer. The funnel's *Jami lidlar* is now board + converted, with a separate *O'quvchiga aylandi* card. The survey submission row (what the parent typed) stays in the submissions register with `lead_id` set to NULL — it is the application record, not the lead.
- [2026-09-22] The two leads already enrolled on the local dev database under the one-day `leads.student_id` design → converted by hand to the new shape (one `lead_conversions` row each, lead rows deleted, column and migration-history row removed); the `LeadStudentLink` migration was deleted before it was ever committed, so no other database has the column.
- [2026-09-22] Menu label: "Savdo va marketing" or EduSchool's "Sotuv va marketing"? → **"Sotuv va marketing"** — the client answered the open question with "eduschool bilan bir xil bo'lsin". UI strings only; the permission key stays `marketing`, file and spec names stay as they are.
- [2026-09-22] Where does the teacher's Mavsumiy baholash (unit C5, spec route `/teacher/seasonal-marks`) live, and does it reuse the admin grid? → **Inside the teacher PWA** (`schoollms.client/src/pages/teacher/ui-web/`) as two screens, `seasonalMarks` (tur → davr → own class/subject pairs) and `seasonalEntry` (bulk entry), reached from Profil → Bo'limlar and listed only when the teacher's `permissions` contain `seasonalMarks`; the admin grid's behaviour is re-implemented there in JSX, not imported → `App.tsx` redirects `/teacher/*` to that separate Vite build, which cannot import the admin TypeScript app; and since the teacher API (§6.6) has only `scope` / `students` / `bulk`, screen 10's list collapses into the prefilled entry screen (emptying both fields deletes a mark; no history, export or per-row delete for teachers).
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal marks (B4): which `year` does a quarterly mark carry — the calendar year or the academic year's start year? → **Whatever the page sends, stored as-is; the page defaults to the calendar year**, so in 2026/2027 Q1–Q2 are `2026` and Q3–Q4 are `2027` (the calendar year the quarter runs in); yearly marks likewise carry the calendar year they are entered in → `quarters` has no year column and the server never converts between calendar and academic years; one rule everyone can see in the picker beats a hidden translation. If the school ever wants "2026/2027" semantics, it is a label change on the page, not a data migration.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal marks (B4): §5.12/§8.6 say a score outside 0–100 is **clamped** server-side; the page **refuses** it ("150 is more likely a typo for 50 or 15") → **the server refuses too** (400 after half-up rounding to 2 decimals; 100.004 → 100 is fine, 100.4 is refused) → a server that clamped would silently turn any other client's typo into a real 100; one rule on both sides of the wire.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal coverage report: `totalStudents` = distinct pupils (§6.6 wording) or pupil × subject slots? → **slots** — one per pupil per (class, subject) the teacher teaches, only current active pupils, only the teacher's half of a split lesson → with heads, a teacher of two subjects in one class shows "60 marked of 30, −30 unmarked"; with slots `total = marked + unmarked` always, the drill-down (one row per pupil × subject) has exactly those counts, and the `min(…,100)` cap is kept but cannot be exceeded.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal coverage for a past period → measured against **today's** schedule and roster → the timetable has no year column and the roster no history; the report says what the data can honestly say.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal "Fanlar kesimida" rows → **today's active pupils of the chosen classes ∪ pupils holding a mark of that period whose class snapshot is one of the chosen classes** (left or moved since) → the page's empty state speaks of "pupils in the chosen classes", and a past period must not lose everyone who left.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal marks: which subjects can a class be marked in → the class-owned timetable only; a subject taught to a class **only through a study group** has no scope → `seasonal_marks.class_id` is a FK to `classes` and a group template stores a group id in that column (§5.12). Revisit if the school marks group subjects seasonally.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal period label for a quarter: §6.6 writes "2 - chorak 2026" (EduSchool) → **"2-chorak 2026"** → the house spelling, and the page's own period caption already uses it; two spellings on one screen would look like two periods.
- [2026-09-22] Seasonal mark comment length → at least 3 code points (§8.6, counted like PostgreSQL's `char_length`, not UTF-16 units) and **at most 2000** (not in the spec) → the column is unbounded `text`; the cap guards against a pasted document and no real comment reaches it.
- [2026-09-22] Scope cut (client: "gamifikatsiya, mavsumiy baholash kabi qismlar juda zarurmas", "agentlar ishni tezlashtirib tugatsin") → **Mavsumiy baholash is parked, not deleted**: its backend (`SeasonalMarksController`, `TeacherSeasonalMarksController`, services, DTOs), admin pages (`pages/admin/seasonal-marks/*`) and teacher-PWA screens stay in the tree but are **wired nowhere** — no admin menu or route, the PWA's menu item and screen registration reverted. The `seasonal_marks` table and the teachers' `seasonalMarks` permission key stay (harmless, and re-enabling is wiring only). **Online entrance test (B3: public token, device lock, expiry sweep) is deferred**: admission and block-test exams run on paper with manual result entry, which is also how EduSchool's block test works; the public page `pages/public/QabulTest*` is written but not routed. Gamification is not started.
- [2026-09-22] Question bank `state` → `unconfigured` when **any** of the three settings (questions per test, time limit, points per correct) is empty, not only `questionsPerTest` as §3.1 says → the publish rule (§8.1) needs all three; a green "Tayyor" badge on a bank that publish then rejects would be a lie. `QuestionBankService.StateOf` is the one definition.
- [2026-09-22] Question import leniencies → the correct-answer cell also accepts lowercase and the Cyrillic look-alikes А/В/С/Е; a blank cell between options closes up; image URLs must be `/uploads/…`; importing the same file twice adds the questions twice (the template's rules sheet warns) → teachers type these sheets by hand in mixed keyboard layouts; rejecting "а" (Cyrillic) as not "a" would fail most real files.
- [2026-09-22] Editing an already-answered question → **allowed, including changing its correct answer**; only removing an option some candidate chose is refused (`option_answered`) → a wrong answer key must be fixable after the sitting.

## 58 mm thermal receipt — XPrinter, two copies (2026-09-22)

- [2026-09-22] How does the browser get the thermal receipt content? → New `GET /api/receipts/{paymentId:guid}` (JSON, `ReceiptPrintDto`) built by the static `ReceiptPrintQuery`, gated exactly like the PDF (`FinanceAction.AcceptPayment` + cashier-sees-only-own = 404) → lines come from `ReceiptService.BuildModel` and every string from `ReceiptText`, so paper and PDF say the same thing; `IReceiptService` untouched because test fakes implement it.
- [2026-09-22] Mapping a receipt line to its abonement month → `ReceiptLine` gained an optional trailing `InvoiceId` (set by `BuildModel`) → the alternative was re-deriving the line list in a second place; an optional last positional parameter breaks no caller.
- [2026-09-22] "Closed / left" per month → `InvoiceDto.Remaining` from `IInvoiceService.ListAsync` (server-computed): `<= 0` → "to'liq yopildi", void invoice → "hisob bekor qilingan", else "qoldi: <Money>" → it is the state **at print time**, not at payment time, so the receipt also prints "Chop etildi: <sana>". A re-print after a later payment or a storno shows the new state.
- [2026-09-22] `@page { size: 58mm auto }` as asked? → **No** — `auto` is only valid alone, Chrome drops `58mm auto` and falls back to A4. The printer module measures one copy and writes `@page { size: 58mm <H>mm; margin: 0 }`, so one page = one copy and the page break between the two copies is where the cutter cuts.
- [2026-09-22] Silent printing → **not attempted in code**; the browser always shows its dialog. The dialog opens by itself right after the payment (hidden iframe `print()`), and the PDF no longer is the main path (secondary link only).
- [2026-09-22] Re-print from Moliya → Tranzaksiyalar → **not built this round** (coordinator: keep only the essentials); the PDF and Telegram actions there are unchanged.

**Kassa kompyuterini sozlash (mijoz uchun, o'zbekcha):**
1. XPrinter drayverini o'rnating va Windows'da uni **asosiy (default) printer** qilib qo'ying.
2. Drayver sozlamasida qog'oz o'lchami: **58 mm** (masalan "58mm x 3276mm" yoki "Receipt 58mm"); kesgich bo'lsa — "har sahifadan keyin kesish" (cut per page).
3. To'lovdan keyin chop etish oynasi o'zi ochiladi. Birinchi marta: printer — **XPrinter**, qog'oz — **58 mm**, chekka (margins) — **Yo'q / None**, masshtab — **100%**, "Sarlavha va pastki yozuv" (headers and footers) — **o'chirilgan**. Chrome bu tanlovni eslab qoladi.
4. Chek ikki nusxada chiqadi: **Maktab nusxasi** va **Ota-ona nusxasi**; oralarida uzuq chiziq (kesish joyi).
5. Oynani umuman ko'rsatmasdan darhol chop etish kerak bo'lsa (ixtiyoriy): kassa kompyuterida Chrome yorlig'iga `--kiosk-printing` bayrog'ini qo'shing (Yorliq → Xususiyatlar → Obyekt oxiriga: ` --kiosk-printing`). Shunda chek to'g'ridan-to'g'ri asosiy printerga ketadi. Bu faqat kassa kompyuteri uchun — boshqa kompyuterlarda qo'shmang.
6. Chek chiqmasa yoki qog'oz tugagan bo'lsa — chek oynasidagi **"Qayta chop etish"** tugmasi.
- [2026-09-22] Exam rules (B2) → a block exam can never be online; an admission exam takes leads only, a block exam pupils/classes only; adding a lead moves it `none → invited`, a saved manual score moves it to `tested` (forward only, never back); removing a participant leaves the lead's status alone; manual results can be entered in draft/published/closed, not cancelled; a draft's sections freeze once any score exists; a participant whose lead was deleted (enrolled) shows as "Nomzod (o'chirilgan)"; an import without `dryRun` only checks. Nothing closes an exam yet — that sweep belonged to the deferred online part.
- [2026-09-22] While online delivery is deferred, **every exam is created as `manual`** (paper, typed results) — `ONLINE_EXAMS_ENABLED = false` in `pages/admin/exams/examLabels.ts` switches the form, and hides the invitation-link, attempt-review, device-unlock and force-finish controls, whose endpoints do not exist yet. Flipping the flag is the whole re-enable on the frontend.
- [2026-09-22] Candidate endpoints (B5) → a separate `LeadCandidatesController` on `api/admin/leads` gated `admission` (the Leads controller is gated `leads` class-wide and must stay unchanged for the frozen board); with an `examId` filter the row shows that exam's participation, not the newest; the card opens for any existing lead; list sorted by name; `enrolled` is refused on the status PATCH (enrolment is its own endpoint and deletes the lead).
- [2026-09-22] Four `FinanceReportsTests` HTTP tests started failing with 400 once the new test classes pushed the shared test database past 600 pupils (`MaxArrearsStudents`). → Those tests (RBAC + JSON shape, not the arithmetic) now scope the pivot to an empty class filter; the arithmetic keeps its own isolated-database tests. Not a product defect — the cap behaves as designed.
- [2026-09-22] Pre-deploy security audit, BLOCKING finding: behind Internet → Cloudflare → Caddy → backend the app saw **Cloudflare's edge IP**, not the visitor, because Caddy appended its own peer to `X-Forwarded-For` and `ForwardLimit` defaults to 1 (rightmost wins). Every IP rate limit — the new public enrolment form (5 / 10 min) and the older `login` (10/min) and `telegram` (20/min) — collapsed into a handful of shared buckets: the form could be pinned shut from anywhere for nothing, and `survey_submissions.ip` recorded the same value for every applicant. → **Caddy is now authoritative**: `trusted_proxies static <Cloudflare ranges>` plus `header_up X-Forwarded-For {client_ip}` emits exactly one entry, and `Program.cs` pins `ForwardLimit = 1` with a comment saying why raising it to 2 would make the value client-spoofable. Refresh the Cloudflare range list when they publish a new one.
- [2026-09-22] Same audit: the origin's `:80` site answered any host, so `http://<server-ip>/ariza/<slug>` served the enrolment form and accepted a parent's name and phone **in cleartext**, outside Cloudflare's TLS and WAF. → `:80` now `abort`s anything that is not a Cloudflare range, localhost or a private network (the proxy's own healthcheck keeps working).
- [2026-09-22] Same audit: the anonymous survey POST inherited Kestrel's 30 MB body limit and every string was normalised before the 50-character cap → `[RequestSizeLimit(16 * 1024)]`, the pattern the import endpoints already use.
- [2026-09-22] Same audit: `Unpublish` cleared the double-publish guard, so unpublish → publish re-sent the whole school a second Telegram message, repeatable by anyone holding `marketing` → publish now skips the fan-out when `telegram_sent_at` is already set (unpublish deliberately never clears it).
- [2026-09-22] Kassa balance/ledger read defect (prod: payments with `cash_box_id` invisible on the Kassa screen). Decisions taken while fixing the read side, write paths untouched: **(a)** a boxed row's money moment is the instant the cash actually moved — payment `received_at`, expense `created_at`, refund `approved_at`; the expense's `on_date` stays the accounting date used by P&L, so a backdated expense appears in the box ledger on the day the drawer was opened, not on `on_date`. **(b)** Only **approved, non-rejected** `student_refunds` count (pending/rejected money never left the drawer); `StudentRefundService` still writes `cash_shift_id`, not `cash_box_id`, so today no refund reaches the screen — the read side is ready for when it does. **(c)** Expenses carry no `method` column and a box is attached only for cash (`AttachCashBoxAsync`), so a boxed expense is booked as `cash`. **(d)** A reversal is signed at the row: a storno payment is negative **in the box that actually handed the money back**, which may be the default box, not the original one (`PaymentService.ReverseAsync` defaults `cashBoxId` to the default box) — the balance follows the data, it does not re-attribute. **(e)** `receiptNo` was added to `CashBoxTransactionRowDto` and is searchable through `q`; the ledger table has no column for it yet (frontend change was limited to `format.ts` this round).
- [2026-09-22] Which cash box does a pupil payment belong to? → **the box whose Kirim form took it** (client: "to'lov qaysi kassada bo'lsa shunda hisoblansin … birniki birinikiga o'tmasin"). The Kirim form now sends `cashBoxId`, and a **storno without an explicit box returns to the original payment's box** instead of the default one. Before this, every payment and every reversal landed on the default box: with one desk nobody noticed, with two the money would have been counted at the wrong desk in both directions. The server keeps "default box" only as a last resort for callers that send no box and for shift-era rows that have none.
- [2026-09-22] Dashboard widget picker ("Vidjetlar") → **EduSchool's list of 25, same groups and order**, then our own dashboard blocks under "Davomat va o'quv". Choice stored in browser `localStorage` per user id (a view preference, no migration). Definitions where EduSchool gives only a name: *Sinfdan chiqarilgan* = active pupil with no class today and at least one closed `class_memberships` row (a transfer is not counted — it has an open row); *Kutayotgan* = no class + `target_grade` set; *Birinchi to'lov* = ever (their 342 matches "ever"), with "this month" as the hint; *Abonement qamrovi* = `studentsActive` of P&L 2.0 for the current month ÷ active pupils. Money widgets reuse the existing finance reports and are shown only to admin/superadmin (the server refuses those reports to anyone else); the lead funnel needs the `leads` permission. **Qarz tufayli bloklangan** and **Ochiq topshiriqlar** are listed but cannot be switched on: we have no debt-block and no staff-task module.
- [2026-09-23] Evening/dormitory attendance — who is "expected"? → pupils with an active `dormitory` subscription on that date, nobody else; others are greyed and the server rejects marks for them → client: "abonementi bo'lsa kechki davomat olish kerak, bo'lmasa kerak emas"; evening lessons are attended only by boarders.
- [2026-09-23] Track ("yo'nalish") groups → a boolean `is_track` on existing `study_groups`, not a new structure → client: "alohida formatlar o'zgartirish kerak emas". A study group still needs a subject, so a track group carries one (any subject is allowed since 2026-09-23).
- [2026-09-23] Boarding marks are saved per section with every eligible pupil (default "present"), so "belgilandi" = a row exists; re-saving updates the row. A parent gets one Telegram notice per absence; changing the status away from absent re-arms the notice.
- [2026-09-23] EduSchool import scope → everything except money: no balances, subscriptions, invoices or payments were written (MAPPING Q-1…Q-4 stay open for the client). With no subscriptions the accrual job creates nothing.

Row 670 was added to the Q-8 list: name ".5444…" with relative ",kjkjj mlkmk" +77777777777.
- [2026-09-23] Incident №939 is recorded against the "Test rejm" duplicate "Azamjonova Mubinaxon" → attached to the active pupil "A'zamjonova Mubinaxon" (8-B), same child; the name differs only by the apostrophe. This keeps the source point totals exact (+81 541 / −17 777).

The "2-…" accounts are separate EduSchool accounts and were imported as they are. The one archived EduSchool teacher (Akbarova Xolidaxon, still listed as the 1-A homeroom) → archived teacher without a login.

Membership date = 2026-09-23 (the source has none). No group teachers were imported, because EduSchool's group list returns nothing.

The morning backup is last year's data and was NOT used → client: "ertalabki baza o'tgan yilgi malumot ekan".

Bus/dormitory/evening abonements are absent from the export, so none were created.

Concurrent payments no longer fail — the second one sees the reduced debt and its remainder becomes advance.
- [2026-09-24] Advance → applied automatically when a new invoice is written (`AdvanceAllocator`, same transaction, same priority), oldest payments first; approved refunds reduce the usable advance. Only allocations are added — no ledger rows, the net balance does not change → client: "Ha, avtomatik yopsin".

The QR at the bottom of the thermal and PDF receipt opens `/r/{token}`, a login-free page (`GET /api/public/receipts/{token}`, 60 req/min per IP) that shows only what the receipt shows plus its CURRENT status (valid / cancelled / reversal). Not by receipt number or id, so other receipts cannot be enumerated. QR base URL = `Receipts__PublicBaseUrl` (prod: https://lms.wunderkindedu.uz); the thermal receipt falls back to the request host, the PDF draws no QR without it.

Still active and pending a decision: 8 pupils with September money but not in the Excel, and Ibrohimova Maftunaxon (Excel 8-B, prod has no class). Backups `pre-tracks_20260924_1742`, `pre-archive246_20260924_1754`.

Safe: 475 of 478 already have the 2026-09 tuition invoice from the EduSchool opening balance and accrual is unique per student×category×month, so September is not billed twice; the 3 "one family" (0 so'm) got 0-amount invoices. Verified after restart: accrual wrote 3 invoices, 0.00 so'm; invoice total and ledger unchanged. Backup `pre-subs-sep_20260924_1806`.

The old A5 `ReceiptDocument` class stays but is no longer used by the service.
- [2026-09-25] Telegram login → Mini App's first sign-in is login+password (`POST /api/tg/link-login`, `login` rate limit), which links that Telegram account; later opens sign in from the Telegram signature without a password. Staff/admin/superadmin/cashier are handed to the web panel inside Telegram with the same token. Phone sharing is not required. Several Telegram accounts per user are allowed "hozircha" (migration `TelegramAccountsManyPerUser` makes `telegram_accounts.user_id` non-unique); one Telegram account still maps to one user; self-unlink removes all of the user's links.
- [2026-09-25] Attendance → "Barcha darslar" mode marks a class's whole day in one save; the marks are written to every lesson (split lessons only get their sub-group), overwriting lessons marked earlier (the confirm dialog says so).
- [2026-09-25] Mini App root cause → production CSP blocked https://telegram.org/js/telegram-web-app.js, so the Mini App never saw Telegram's initData and fell back to browser login (nothing linked, password every time, then a /login flash in the web panel). Fixed: script-src allows telegram.org; frame-ancestors allows only self + Telegram (web.telegram.org iframes the Mini App), X-Frame-Options removed; the staff hand-off also stores `user`. HttpClient logs lowered to Warning — they printed the bot token in request URLs.
- [2026-09-25] Receipt reprint → student card "Chop etish" prints the 58 mm receipt in 2 copies exactly as first printed (`/api/receipts/{id}?original=true`): line status from allocations up to that payment's time, "Chop etildi" = the payment's audit create time, same QR token. One deliberate difference: a payment reversed later still carries the "BEKOR QILINGAN" stamp, so a cancelled payment cannot be reprinted as valid.
- [2026-09-25] Phone format everywhere → one `PhoneInput` (+998 fixed, 9 digits typed) and `formatPhone` for display, stored as `+998 97 666 66 66`; old rows are not rewritten, display formats them → server already keys phones by last 9 digits (`PhoneUtil.Key`), so no migration needed; non-Uzbek legacy values are shown as stored.
- [2026-09-25] Student card → new "Abonementlar" tab (admin/director only) reusing the billing subscription API and modals; opened from the card, the form shows the student read-only instead of the student search.
- [2026-09-25] "One active subscription per category" → the form now disables categories whose existing subscription overlaps the chosen period (same rule as server `subscription_overlap`); the server check was already there, no DB constraint added.
- [2026-09-25] "Fixed abonement price" → new nullable `fee_categories.monthly_amount`, set on Moliya → Toifalar. When set, the server opens every new subscription at that price (request amount ignored) and rejects any manual amount change except aligning to the current category price (`amount_fixed`). A price change affects only new subscriptions. Tuition keeps its class-fee price (UI-locked on the student card only, server unchanged — existing tests rely on explicit tuition amounts). Category without a price on the student card → cannot open, "set a price first".
- [2026-09-25] Staff access roles → new `access_roles` table + `users.access_role_id`; permissions are granted to a role, staff get a role. Members' `users.permissions` is kept as a copy of the role's list (rewritten on assign / role edit) so auth claims, `AdminPerm` and the menu stay unchanged. Existing staff are NOT auto-migrated into roles: they keep their per-user permissions ("Rolsiz · N ta ruxsat") until a superadmin assigns a role. Role CRUD and assignment are superadmin-only (as permission editing was); a role with members cannot be deleted. Teachers, cashier, admin, superadmin keep their system roles — access roles apply to staff accounts only. EduSchool's "Faqat boss ko'ra oladi" flag and per-section sub-actions were not copied (no such concept here yet).
- [2026-09-25] Attendance marking (`/admin/attendance/mark`) showed only the two reasons bound to red/amber, so other catalogue reasons (Kasal, Oilaviy sabab, Kech qoldi ...) could not be picked → the three colours stay; an amber row now gets a small reason dropdown with every catalogue reason except the red one (late reasons included — there is no other place on this screen to mark them), defaulting to the server's amber reason; the column button resets everyone to that default → client asked for three colours, and the catalogue list comes from the daily endpoint itself so the screen still needs only the `attendance` permission.
- [2026-09-25] Attendance reasons settings → name + delete only (client: reasons are for absent pupils only, no code, no late flag). Short code is generated from the name (existing codes kept). The "Kech qoldi" row (isLate + name contains "kech") is hidden and preserved on save, re-created if missing, so the journal/Mini App late mark keeps working. isLate rows without "kech" in the name (prod had Kasal/Sababli/Sababsiz flagged late by mistake) show as absence reasons and are corrected on the next save.
- [2026-09-25] Student search by phone → only when the search text is phone-shaped (digits, spaces, + - ( ), ≥3 digits); compared without separators against parent and student phone. Text with letters searches names only, as before.
- [2026-09-25] Finance by role (client: "hamma narsa role bilan boshqarilsin", confirmed: without approvals) → staff whose role has the "finance" permission get an internal role `financestaff` per request (Program.cs), accepted by `Roles.FinanceStaff`, `CashierOrAdmin` and the admin-level FinanceMatrix rows. Director-only approvals (discount/expense/refund approval, expense threshold) stay superadmin; self-approval stays forbidden. Staff WITHOUT the permission remain 403 — the 18 SPEC §4.3 tests now assert that. Role/permission management stays superadmin-only (prevents self-escalation).
- [2026-09-26] Page-level role access (client: menu or single submenu, levels none / view / full; confirmed choices: buttons stay and a denied write shows a message; old roles migrate as full; menu + page closed, shared reference reads stay open) → permission unit = menu page (`navigation.ts` is the catalog, "Future" hidden). Stored in the existing `permissions` list without a migration: `"/admin/finance/debtors|view"` page grants plus derived section keys (`finance` = full, `finance:view` = view) that the server already enforces. Legacy roles (section keys only) read as full on every page of the section. Server: writes need the full key (AdminPerm), `finance:view` → internal `financeviewer` role that reads every finance endpoint while `ViewOnlyWriteGuard` rejects writes; director approvals unchanged. `ViewAccessSweepTests` walks every parameterless admin/cash GET and requires it to open for a view-only staff member, except login/password exports and branches (superadmin).
- [2026-09-26] Staff salary: "exactly one of teacher_id / employee_user_id on a salary expense" → DB enforces AT MOST one (`ck_expenses_one_salary_recipient`) plus `ck_expenses_employee_only_salary`; FK employee_user_id → users RESTRICT, deleting a paid staff account → 409 → existing salary rows with no recipient (generic `POST /api/admin/expenses`) must stay valid, and paid salary is financial history.
- [2026-09-26] Staff salary proration → first month = salary × days from start (incl.) / days in month, rounded to 2 decimals half-up; empty start date accrues from the period start, like teachers without a start date → mirrors `TeacherSalaryCalc.StartDateOf` semantics; money column is numeric(14,2).
- [2026-09-26] Staff salary endpoints (`api/admin/staff/{id}/salary-*`) → finance gate only (FinanceStaff + FinanceRole), no `AdminPerm("staff")` → spec: staff with `finance` may pay; the staff section key alone is not a money permission (F3.05).
- [2026-09-26] `GET /api/admin/staff` stays open to every staff account (reference list) but returns salary 0 / start date "" unless the caller is admin/superadmin, a finance role, or has `staff`/`staff:view` → salary is money (F0.02); every screen that lists staff salaries already has one of those.
- [2026-09-26] `ExpenseDto` gains `employeeUserId` / `employeeName`, `CreateExpenseRequest` gains `employeeUserId` (the generic expense endpoint validates it like `teacherId`); salary report export's first column is now "Xodim" next to the new "Lavozim" → the expense list must show who a staff salary went to.

Rollar: granting "Xodimlar" emits both `staff` and `teachers` (`PAGE_EXTRA_SECTIONS` in `lib/access.ts`). A legacy `teachers`-only role, or a saved grant for the retired `/admin/teachers` page, still opens Xodimlar at runtime with teachers only; the role editor does NOT add the page on re-save → keeps today's access without escalating a teachers-only role to staff-account management.

Old `TeachersPage.tsx` / `StaffPage.tsx` kept on disk, no longer routed.
- [2026-09-26] Track group's existing arbitrary subject (prod: 5 tracks) — keep or clear? → migration TrackGroupsAsClasses clears it (members' copy too); `ck_study_groups_subject` = track ⇔ no subject → doc allowed it and a leftover subject would leak into group-subject logic; members/classes/teachers untouched; Down() puts the first subject back (original not restorable).
- [2026-09-26] Composite FK members(group_id, subject_id) → study_groups(id, subject_id) with nullable subject → kept IN THE DATABASE (MATCH SIMPLE skips NULL), removed from the EF model, plus a plain group_id FK → EF cannot have a nullable alternate key; same "DB-only" pattern as ux_study_groups_name_active.
- [2026-09-26] "One active track group per pupil" — DB or service? → service (StudyGroupService add/candidates/update/transfer), no DB index → a partial index needs is_track on members; race window is two admins at once, acceptable.
- [2026-09-26] Track-group name uniqueness → service-level among active tracks (no new DB index) → avoids a deploy-time failure if prod already had duplicate names.

- [2026-09-26] O'qituvchi jadvali and teacher portal lists → still show ALL classes (plus track groups) → an old 9-A timetable is still paid by salary; hiding it would make paid lessons invisible.
- [2026-09-26] Pupil timetable (portal/Mini App) of a track member → home-class lessons hidden only in weeks where the track group has a timetable → avoids double timetables without blanking pupils before track timetables are entered.
- [2026-09-26] Ordinary (non-track) groups in Davomat belgilash → not added (even with the switch on) → they are split lessons of classes; only classes and track groups are "homerooms" for daily marking.
- [2026-09-26] Chat channels for track groups → unchanged (still behind group_lessons_enabled) → chat is outside the timetable/attendance/journal scope of this build.
- [2026-09-26] Salary / teacher activity with track lessons → track lessons now count regardless of the switch (they are real lessons); old 9–11 class timetables still count too until the school removes them → client must drop 9–11 class timetables once track timetables are entered, or lessons are paid twice.
- [2026-09-26] "Held lesson" for ATTENDANCE STATISTICS → LessonNote.Conducted OR a daily_attendance_marks row for the same (date, owner id, subject, period) — `HeldLessons` helper used by attendance analytics, dashboard, class analytics/ratings, pupil profile (portal/Mini App), student profile attendance, discipline report, student evaluation, journal "conducted" list; on a marked lesson a pupil with no journal row counts as PRESENT (not unchecked) → the marking screen's own rule; teacher work/salary (TeacherActivityReport, SubjectProgress, salary) keep Conducted-only, nothing is written to lesson_notes.
- [2026-09-26] Attendance marking → two buttons (client): K = present with an optional late reason underneath (isLate reasons, default "Keldi" = no mark), Y = absent with a reason underneath (default Sababsiz, then the other non-late reasons). Reasons settings shows two lists (Kelmadi / Kech keldi sabablari); an empty late list keeps "Kech qoldi" because the teacher journal and Mini App use it. Stored data unchanged: late = isLate reason (counts present), any other reason = absent — analytics already follow this.
- [2026-09-26] Kunlik davomat report when a subject occurs twice in a day → journal rows matched by subject AND period; the per-lesson student list takes an optional `period` and shows a late pupil as "Keldi · <reason>" (absent=false) → the table already counted late as present; the list contradicted it.
- [2026-09-26] Dashboard "Bugungi davomat" per period → a lesson marked on Davomat belgilash counts its whole roster as checked (no row = present), expected size includes marked classes/track groups → same HeldLessons rule as analytics; before, present pupils showed as "tekshirilmagan".
- [2026-09-26] Dashboard "Dars qoldirayotgan o'quvchilar" → counts only unexcused reasons (AttendanceAnalytics definition: name contains "sababsiz" or negative points) and "Oxirgi kelgan" also uses marked lessons where the pupil had no absence row → the card and its comment already promised "faqat sababsiz"; with the Y+reason button excused absences are now common.
- [2026-09-26] Attendance-discipline report filtered by a track group id → left as is (rows stay per homeroom class; the page's picker lists classes only) → report answers "which class loses points", track lessons are already inside the class rows.
- [2026-09-26] Daily marks for a split lesson (subGroup 1/2 with the same subject+period) → NOT fixed: daily_attendance_marks has no sub_group column, saving one half marks both halves → needs a migration; left for a client decision (no such lessons in local data).
- [2026-09-26] Marking a track lesson on a date BEFORE a pupil joined the group → not fixed: marking screen and analytics use today's active members, profile/class performance use membership windows, so such a pupil's mark is ignored by the latter → only retroactive marking is affected; local test data memberships were back-dated to 2026-09-01 for the check.
- [2026-09-26] StaffSalaryMigrationTests Down test broke after BoardingAttendanceReason (ccdbdc5) → the test now measures Down/Up from its own migration (20260926061213_StaffSalary), not from head → the assertion is about THIS migration's columns; head moves with every later migration. Assertion strength unchanged.
- [2026-09-26] Back-dated attendance for track groups → the marking screen lists the group members active ON the chosen date (same membership-date rule the reports use). One-off data migration TrackMembersFromYearStart moves joined_on of first-time active track members to quarter-1 start (prod: 117 of 118 rows, 2026-09-24 → 2026-09-02) because the groups were only entered into the system on 2026-09-24; pupils with earlier track history are left alone. Down is a no-op.
- [2026-09-26] MCP SDK version → ModelContextProtocol.AspNetCore 2.2.0 (latest stable, net10.0 lib), pinned exactly → the protocol moves fast; a floating version could change wire behaviour on an unrelated rebuild.
- [2026-09-26] MCP transport session mode → Stateless (SDK default for the 2026-07-28 revision; older initialize-handshake clients still work) → no session affinity, every request re-authenticates and re-checks the user, which is exactly the freshness rule we want.
- [2026-09-26] Tool area permission → a tool requires the section key (full or `:view`) even though AdminPerm lets any staff GET any section → the doc says "never more than the user sees in the web panel"; the panel hides pages without the key, so the stricter reading was taken.
- [2026-09-26] Money inside non-finance tools (pupil balance, subscriptions, staff salary) → returned only with finance/finance:view, debt filters refused otherwise → spec: "finance needs finance access".
- [2026-09-26] app_ro scope → SELECT on every table EXCEPT mcp_auth_codes/mcp_tokens (hashed OAuth secrets) → tools never need them; least privilege beats the literal "all tables".
- [2026-09-26] app_ro extra locks → role default `default_transaction_read_only=on` and `statement_timeout=30s` → a stray grant still cannot write and a runaway AI query cannot hog the DB.
- [2026-09-26] mcp_audit → append-only for app_rw (listed in init-roles.sql step 5) → what an AI read must not be editable afterwards; no retention job yet.
- [2026-09-26] DCR redirect URIs → https anywhere or http on loopback (port may differ, RFC 8252); custom schemes (cursor://…) refused → MCP auth spec; a client needing a custom scheme is an open question.
- [2026-09-26] OAuth scope → a single `school.read`; whatever scope the client asks for, that one is granted → there is nothing else to grant; clients that send no scope still work.
- [2026-09-26] Token lifetimes → access 1 h, refresh 30 d rotating; refresh reuse or code replay revokes the whole grant → doc numbers + RFC 6819 theft detection.
- [2026-09-26] OAuth login form → reuses the "login" rate-limit policy (10/min/IP, shared with /api/auth/login); DCR 10/h/IP; token 30/min/IP; /mcp 120/min per grant → "rate-limited like /api/auth/login".
- [2026-09-26] Discipline scores tool → re-implements the 10-line formula of DisciplineController.BuildScoresAsync (private) instead of refactoring the controller → additive-only rule; comment points to the source.
- [2026-09-26] BoardingAttendanceService/InvoiceService/ExpenseService/DiscountService/SeasonalMarkService constructed with write-side deps (null Telegram, LedgerService/AuditService on the RO context) → only their read methods are called; any write would throw (ReadOnlyDatabase interceptor) and be refused by app_ro anyway.
- [2026-09-26] "AI ulanishlar" API at api/admin/mcp (superadmin) → added to ViewAccessSweepTests.RoleLocked like branches → it is not a page a role can grant.
- [2026-09-26] Local E2E → added DB_RO_PASSWORD to the LOCAL .env and ran init-roles.sql on the local DB; backend recreated with --no-deps (database container untouched) → needed to exercise app_ro locally.
- [2026-09-26] Read-only EF context → an AppDbContext built from app_ro options with a SaveChanges-refusing interceptor (ReadOnlyDatabase), NOT a DbContext subclass → a second DbContext type made every `dotnet ef migrations` command fail with "More than one DbContext was found".
- [2026-09-26] MCP security review H1 → OAuth redirects only to `Mcp:AllowedRedirectHosts` (default claude.ai, claude.com, chatgpt.com, chat.openai.com; https, default port, exact IdnHost) or http loopback; raw URI must equal its canonical AbsoluteUri → an open DCR + consent page is otherwise a phishing kit; subdomains are NOT implied (add them explicitly).
- [2026-09-26] MCP review M1 → app_rw pool capped at 40, app_ro at 8 (+ CONNECTION LIMIT 8); /mcp concurrency 2 per connection / 6 per process, no queue; class_performance cached 5 min server-wide → server max_connections=60 must hold app_rw + app_ro + migrator/backup/psql.
- [2026-09-26] MCP review M3 → page-grant roles are checked per tool page (McpAreas mirrors navigation.ts, a test pins the keys); legacy section-key roles keep section checks; money inside non-finance tools still keys off the derived `finance`/`finance:view` section → matches how lib/access.ts derives section keys from pages.
- [2026-09-26] MCP review M4 → any SaveChanges that modifies users.password_hash revokes that user's MCP grants (AppDbContext override) → covers every password path, present and future, without touching each controller; login (email) change does not revoke.
- [2026-09-26] MCP review M6 → app_ro privileges are an explicit table allow-list + column allow-lists (users, school_meta, guardians) in ONE SQL function (mcp_apply_app_ro_grants) called by the migration and init-roles.sql; mcp_* tables, audit_logs, questions, invitations, device/telegram tokens, cameras, chat are closed; new tables closed by default.
- [2026-09-26] MCP review L7 → audit retention 1 year via SECURITY DEFINER mcp_prune_audit (≥365 days hard floor), swept at most daily on audit write; if the audit row cannot be written the tool returns an error instead of data → the retention length is a CLIENT DECISION still to confirm.
- [2026-09-26] MCP review L2 → the read-only connection is hardened in code (pool ≤ 8, timeouts, no error detail, `-c default_transaction_read_only=on`) and self-tested at startup; EF interceptor passes only SELECT/WITH → MCP stays off if app_ro is not what it claims.
- [2026-09-26] MCP migration regenerated as 20260926150755_McpReadOnly (audit ip/user_agent) → it was uncommitted; the LOCAL database's first McpReadOnly tables/history row were dropped by hand so the new one applies cleanly (local only).
- [2026-09-26] app_ro allow-list includes test_questions (homework quiz questions incl. correct index) → the pupil card's assignment scores load them (found by the real-data E2E); entrance-exam questions/banks/options stay closed.
- [2026-09-27] Mini App parent tabs call `/api/student/*` with `?studentId=` → for a parent the id is honoured only when it names one of their OWN children (guardianship, then legacy phone); a foreign id is still silently ignored, not 404 → keeps the old contract, so nothing about a stranger's child leaks and old callers keep working (`GuardianAccess.ParentTargetAsync`).
- [2026-09-27] Receipt "download" inside Telegram → the bot sends the PDF to the caller's own Telegram chat (`POST /api/tg/receipts/{id}/send`), browser keeps the file download → blob downloads do nothing in Telegram's Android WebView; `WebApp.downloadFile` would need an unauthenticated receipt URL.
- [2026-09-27] Pupil accounts open the parent panel (parents often use the child's login) → the panel skips the parent-only `/api/tg/parent/children` for them instead of showing its 403.
- [2026-09-27] Which week is "current" on a Sunday (weeks are Mon–Sat, Sunday fell into none and the code returned week 1) → the week that started the Monday before (calendar week Mon–Sun) → a principled answer that never jumps ahead or back to the quarter start (`PortalSchedule.WeekOf`).
