- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — `GET /api/hr/me/tasks` serves two old clients → `?date=` (or no query) = the Mini App day list `{date, items}`; any of `limit/cursor/withTotal/dateFrom/dateTo/status/overdue/accepted/q/sort` without `date` = the panel page `Page<HrTask>`; `GET /api/hr/me/day` is an explicit alias of the day list → both frontends were ported to this one path (ASSUMPTIONS above).
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — `/api/hr/me/*` error codes → the `hr_` prefix is dropped (`proof_required`, `task_not_owned`, `task_not_editable` …) like the old Mini App router, EXCEPT `hr_not_employee` (spec'd with the prefix); panel endpoints keep full codes (`hr_manager_cycle` …); every error body is `{code, message}` → both old clients match on these codes.
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — Telegram texts use `parse_mode=HTML` (old texts have `<b>`) → added `TelegramService.SendHtmlAsync` + `SendVoiceAsync` (additive; existing callers untouched); the old inline "✅ Tasdiqlash" callback button is NOT sent → our bot polls `message` updates only (no callback handler); acceptance stays in the Mini App/panel. No link buttons either: the old texts point to «Topshiriqlarim» (the bot's menu button).
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — manager voice-note length → no ffmpeg/ffprobe on our server; the attachment upload takes an optional `durationSeconds` form field (the recorder knows it) and refuses > 300 s; without it the length is unknown (old behaviour when ffprobe was missing); no m4a transcoding.
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — upload type rule → the old blocklist (html/xhtml/svg/xml/js MIME) PLUS a blocked-extension list (`.html .svg .js .xml …`), everything else allowed (zip, audio, office…), 10 MB, 5 proofs/task; stored as `uploads/hr/{proofs|attachments}/yyyy/MM/<guid>.<ext>` → `UploadGuard`'s allowlist would refuse archives/audio the old system accepted; `/uploads` serves by extension, so the extension check is what stops stored XSS.
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — deadline reminders idempotency → per task in `hr_task_reminders (task_id, offset_minutes)` (old table), not `hr_job_runs` → reminders fire many times a day per task; `hr_job_runs` (job, local date) guards generate / morning / digest / close_overdue / clean_drafts.
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — digest recipients → admin/superadmin and staff with `hrTasks` or `hrTasks:view` that have a `telegram_accounts` row; scope: admin/superadmin and HR directors = whole organisation, others = their subtree, a panel user without an HR row = nothing → §7 + the old supervisors rule (admin/superadmin = all, manager = subtree).
- [2026-09-27] HR tasks backend — `hr_employees.user_id` FK → RESTRICT (deleting a user with an HR row fails; the HR row is deactivated instead) → task history must not vanish with an account; the staff and teacher delete endpoints answer a readable 409 for such a user instead of a 23503 → 500.
- [2026-09-28] "Odamlar ro'yxatiga o'qituvchilarni ham tiqsin" — how? → every active teacher and staff/cashier/admin user gets an HR row automatically (scheduler loop, every 5 min); superadmins and archived teachers skipped → teachers could not get tasks or be placed in the tree; the system superadmin is not a person.
- [2026-09-28] "Lavozimni o'zidan pastlari uchun arxitektura" → the existing subordination tree (HR → Topshiriqlar → Xodimlar → Daraxt, managers per employee) is the architecture; no separate position-rank model → it existed and was only empty of teachers; a rank model would be a second, conflicting hierarchy.
- [2026-09-28] Synced people without a phone → `hr_employees.phone` made nullable (19 teachers + 2 staff have none) → phone is only a label for existing users; Telegram goes by user_id.
- [2026-09-28] Auto-sync never deactivates → an archived teacher keeps an active HR row until a director fires them in HR → firing cancels open tasks; that stays a human decision.
- [2026-09-28] Deleting a teacher/staff with an HR row → allowed when the row has no tasks/rules (row removed), 409 otherwise → with everyone synced the old unconditional 409 would block every delete.
- [2026-09-28] LMS bot contact share → only `contact.user_id == from.id` accepted; archived students not matched → a forwarded contact card let anyone receive another family's receipts and discipline notices.
- [2026-09-28] "HR bo'limida xodim qo'shish shartmas" → removed "+ Xodim" from the HR panel, name/phone read-only in its edit dialog, sync copies the LMS name onto every HR row (and fills a missing phone) → Boshqaruv is the one place people are managed; an HR name that never follows it would go stale (e.g. "Ulug'bek" vs "Mirzoulug'bek Yusupov").
- [2026-09-28] "Lavozimlar ham bu qismda boshqarilmasin" → "Lavozimlar" button and the edit-dialog position select removed from HR; sync mirrors the LMS "Lavozim" (teacher → O'qituvchi, staff → users.position; empty → HR position kept) → Boshqaruv's positions are richer ("Akademik direktor", "Buxgalter") than the old HR's "Xodim"/"Director", and an empty one must not wipe a director's label.
- [2026-09-28] "Tahlil natijalari ham xodimlar akkauntida bo'lishi kerak" → Dars tahlili results in the teacher's Telegram Mini App: a self-loading "Dars tahlilim" card on the Bugun tab (average, last 3) + full-screen sheet (list, one analysis with criteria, AI summary); no PDF → the web teacher page and PWA already had it, the Mini App (where teachers live) did not; a 7th tab would not fit 320 px; only teachers are observed, so staff accounts get nothing.
- [2026-09-28] "Topshiriqlar va asl xodim profili aralashib ishlashi kerak, umumiy menular sifatida" → Mini App no longer shows staff a tasks-only panel; staff/admin/cashier open their own CRM panel and "Mening topshiriqlarim" is a top-level menu there (moved out of HR); teachers keep the "Topshiriqlar" tab added to their own panel → a tasks-only panel hid the CRM menus staff work in.
- [2026-09-28] "O'qituvchilar accountida o'zining oylik maoshi ko'rinmasin" → hard rule: `/api/teacher/salary` is always 403; the Mini App "Maosh" tab, the PWA salary screen/profile row and the web teacher "Maosh" menu removed; the `salary` teacher key retired (not granted, not offered) → the key was on by default for everyone; a per-teacher switch would not hold "teachers do not see it".
- [2026-09-28] "Mini app button emas, botning ichida bitta button bo'lsin — ota-onada ham" → persistent reply-keyboard button "🏫 Panelni ochish" (web_app) for every linked account + bot-wide menu button set to "commands"; keyboard launches get no initData, so the URL carries a personal key (256-bit, SHA-256 stored in telegram_accounts.panel_key_hash, rotated on every keyboard send, 90-day life, dies on unlink) traded at POST /api/tg/auth/key; unlinked chats get an inline "🔐 Panelga kirish" (initData) and the keyboard arrives after linking; one-time rollout message to all linked accounts on first start → client chose "Telegram ichida" (option 1) incl. parents; the key opens admin panels too, hence stored hashed and revocable instead of a stateless token.
- [2026-09-28] Which applied sickness discounts the payment form's green notice offers to cancel → those created in the last 30 days (server returns all standing ones; the form filters) → cancelling is for mistakes caught soon; older ones would clutter the payment form for years.
- [2026-09-29] "Abonementlar sentabrdan boshlanishi kerak, EduSchool bilan bir xil" → EduSchool bus/dorm/evening subscriptions start 2026-09-01 with `accrue_from = 2026-10-01`; students without a September invoice (2 on prod) stay on 1 October → September money is already inside the imported September tuition invoice, so accruing September again would bill it twice.
- [2026-10-06] How to count days when a student leaves mid-month with an abonement scheduled "Butun oy" (all prod plans) → lesson days Mon–Sat minus holidays → the client chose "dars kunlari, yakshanba va bayramlar sanalmaydi"; the sick discount keeps its own (calendar) rule for "Butun oy".
- [2026-10-07] When may accrual bill a month that holds a void invoice → only for a subscription created, or with its amount/end date changed, after the void → that is how staff re-price (end + void + new abonement, prod: Akramov); an untouched subscription keeps a deliberate void, and a note-only save must not revive it.
- [2026-10-07] Imported bus/dorm/evening abonements of 10 students whose September invoice is tuition only → `starts_on` back to 2026-10-01, `accrue_from` cleared (client: "Sanani 01.10 ga qaytarish") → their September is in no invoice and EduSchool did not bill it, so 01.09 only misled; money unchanged. Anvarova's ended evening abonement (01.09–17.09) is left as it is.
- [2026-10-07] An abonement starting mid-month (client: "o'qigan kuniga qarab hisoblashi kerak") → the start month is billed by lesson days from the start date (Mon–Sat, holidays excluded, the abonement's own schedule if it has one); the end month stays full (Q12) → matches the cashier's own 338 000 for Jaloliddinov and the "Chiqib ketdi" rule the client chose on 2026-10-06; an end-month proration would double with "Chiqib ketdi".
- [2026-10-07] Which holidays to put on prod → the official days off (01.09, 01.10, 08.12, 01.01, 08.03, 21.03, 09.05); 14.01 is not a day off; Ramazon/Qurbon hayit are lunar and announced yearly, so the school adds them → no journal or attendance record existed on 01.09 / 01.10, so nothing is hidden.
- [2026-10-07] Fines in the teacher's cabinet (client request #6) → Mini App "Bugun" gets a "Jarimalar" card (own fines, cancelled ones struck through with the reason; hidden while there are none) instead of a new tab → the tab bar already holds 5–6 tabs and the card pattern (lesson analysis, news) is how the Bugun screen grows.
- [2026-10-07] Who is told about a fine, and when → the employee, on Telegram, when a fine is written AND when it is cancelled; bonuses send nothing (not asked); staff who are not teachers get the message but have no cabinet list yet → the request names fines and teachers; a cancellation the teacher is not told about would leave the first message standing.
- [2026-10-07] Should a fine be deducted from the salary figure → not in this change: fines still do not enter `SalaryLedger`/salary rates (HR-09 payroll is a separate, unbuilt module) → the request asked for visibility and a message only; changing payroll numbers is its own decision.
- [2026-10-07] Letter results on certificates (client request #3, "harfli holatda") → a certificate type gets "Daraja qo'yiladi" plus an optional ordered list ("A1, A2, B1, B2, C1, C2"); with a list the level must be one of it, without one it is free text (≤30); a type can carry a score, a level or both → CEFR, Cambridge A*–U and Pass/Merit/Distinction all fit one model, and the order lets "Natijalar" rank levels (shown as a distribution, never averaged).
- [2026-10-07] What "hozircha necha balli" means → the child's certificates with their results, and per type the latest result, in the Mini App "Bosh" screen for the student and the parent (`GET /api/student/certificates`), not behind the parent progress switch → the request sits under "Sertifikatlar va natijalar"; grade averages already show in "Baholar"; block-test scores and gamification points can join the card later if the client means those.
- [2026-10-07] Feedback departments and routing (client request #1) → seven departments seeded (O'quv bo'limi, Moliya, Ovqatlanish, Transport, Yotoqxona, HR, Rahbariyat), renamable/switchable in "Bo'limlar"; the sender may pick one (optional), the inbox can re-route and name a responsible person; a new item reaches the department's people by bot, or every admin and inbox staff when it has nobody → the client named departments and a responsible person but no list; a missed complaint is worse than one extra message.
- [2026-10-07] Feedback statuses → "new" stays the stored value and reads "Qabul qilindi", then "in_progress" (Jarayonda) and "resolved" (Hal qilindi); any status can be set again (re-open); the sender gets a bot message on every status change and on an answer; internal notes stay inside → keeps old rows and the admin bell working and gives the three statuses the client listed.
- [2026-10-07] Who works the inbox → the existing "feedback" permission (admins always); a department's responsible person without it gets the bot message and is marked "ruxsati yo'q" in the department screen → the permission model stays one rule; no anonymous sending (the school needs to answer someone).
- [2026-10-07] Several lesson analysis sheets (client request #5) → a sheet (varaq) owns its sections and criteria; the first sheet holds everything that existed; a new sheet starts empty or as a copy (sections + active criteria); the observer picks the sheet on the form (the default sheet is preselected) and can change it only before the first answer; scoring stays yes/no → the client gave no rule tying sheets to subjects or teacher categories, and a manual choice covers every case without guessing.
- [2026-10-07] Editing a sheet after observations exist → each answer stores the criterion text and section title it was given against (backfilled for old answers); the PDF, AI summary and teacher view read the stored text → before this, editing a criterion silently rewrote what past observations said.
- [2026-10-07] Who edits sheets and sections → the existing criteria gate (admin, or staff with the "lessonAnalysisCriteria" flag plus full "lessonAnalysis"); criteria still move only inside their own sheet → one rule for everything that changes the checklist.
- [2026-10-07] O'rindosh ustoz — who assigns, and where? → Admin/staff with the `teachers` key (same as teacher attendance), on HR → "O'rindosh ustozlar": pick a date and the absent teacher, name a free teacher per lesson; busy teachers are disabled → the people who mark absence are the ones who arrange cover.
- [2026-10-07] O'rindosh ustoz — pay? → The substitute gets +1 lesson at their own category rate (bonus % applies); the absent teacher −1 lesson, unless that whole day is already marked "absent" (then it is already deducted) → no double deduction; shown on Oylik hisoblash and in the teacher's salary detail.
- [2026-10-07] O'rindosh ustoz — journal access? → The substitute may write that class+subject journal only on the covered date, and read it (notes, roster) for 30 days after; the activity report credits the covered lesson (plan, mark, grades) to the substitute → minimal access, no permanent widening.
- [2026-10-07] O'rindosh ustoz — re-assigning / cancelling? → A new substitute for the same lesson cancels the old row; cancel is soft (cancelled_at/by), the bot tells the substitute both ways → closed months stay explainable.
- [2026-10-07] So'rovnoma (client request #7) — what is it? → A new "Poll" module (not the existing `Survey`, which is the leads enrolment form): the admin builds a poll for parents or teachers from NPS 0–10, 1–5 stars, single choice and free text questions; a question rates the school, each teacher of the parent's child, or each HR director/manager; delivered in the Mini App (card on Bugun / Bosh sahifa) → reuses the Telegram-only channel and gives per-person NPS for KPI.
- [2026-10-07] Poll anonymity → anonymous by default: the response keeps only a SHA-256 of (poll, respondent) to stop double answers, plus the child's class for the class breakdown; non-anonymous polls keep the student/teacher id and show names on text answers → honest NPS needs parents to feel safe.
- [2026-10-07] Parents answer per child → one answer sheet per child (a parent with two children answers twice, rating each child's own teachers); "required" on a per-person question means at least one person rated → nobody has to rate every teacher.
- [2026-10-07] Bot announcement of a poll → never automatic; an explicit "Botdan e'lon qilish" button on an open poll, with the recipient count in the confirm dialog, once per poll → a broadcast to every parent is outward-facing and must be a deliberate act.
- [2026-10-07] KPI formula → per active HR employee per month, 0–100 components: HR tasks on time, lesson analysis mean %, journal conducted % (quarter), parents' NPS and staff NPS mapped (NPS+100)/2, fines 100−20 each, attendance 100−10/absent−3/late; the total is the weighted mean of the components that have a score, and only when at least one work component (tasks, lesson analysis, journal, NPS) has one — fines/attendance alone would hand a perfect 100 to someone nothing is known about; default weights 25/25/10/15/10/10/5, editable → the client named the goal ("samaradorlik ko'rsatkichi") but no formula; every input already exists in the system.
- [2026-10-07] Who sees polls and KPI → two new permission keys, `polls` and `kpi`, both gated for reading too (colleagues' scores and parents' free-text answers are not for every staff member) → follows the admission/HR pattern.
- [2026-10-07] Ishga qabul (client request #2) — where do "vakansiya qoldirgan xodimlar" come from? → A public page `/vakansiya` (all open vacancies) and `/vakansiya/{slug}` (apply form with a CV), same protections as the enrolment form (honeypot, per-IP rate limit, same answer for a repeat), plus "Nomzod qo'shish" for people who call or are recommended → the client named the section but not the channel; a link works in Telegram, Instagram and job sites.
- [2026-10-07] Hiring stages → exactly the client's five (Ma'lumot/CV → Test → Demo → HR suhbat → Direktor suhbati), each with an optional 0–100 score and a comment; moving forward or back or skipping a stage is allowed (a cook needs no demo lesson), hiring only after the director's interview; rejection needs a reason and can be undone ("Qayta ko'rib chiqish") → a board with no locked order fits a small school's HR.
- [2026-10-07] Adaptatsiya → 30 days from the hire date, 11 starting steps (documents, accounts, tour, mentor, team, LMS training, observing, first lessons with the mentor, HR check-in day 14, lesson analysis day 21, director's final review day 30) with due dates; editable in "Adaptatsiya bosqichlari"; a hire keeps their own copy; finishing requires every step done → the client gave the shape (30 days, 11 steps) but not the content; these are the usual school onboarding steps and are editable.
- [2026-10-07] CV storage → `uploads/private/recruitment/`, never served statically (Program.cs blocks `/uploads/private`), downloaded only through the gated admin endpoint → a CV is personal data; the rest of `/uploads` is public by unguessable name, which is not enough here.
- [2026-10-07] Recruitment messages → none: no Telegram to applicants or staff, and hiring does not create an LMS account or HR employee → the HR cut-over is on hold (old HR bot live, LMS HR messages off on prod); the account is still made in Xodimlar when the person starts.
- [2026-10-07] Who sees recruitment → a new permission key `recruitment` (HR group), reads gated too → applicants' contacts and CVs.
- [2026-10-07] Teacher Mini App must look the same for every teacher (client: "o'qituvchi bo'lsa bo'ldi, hammasida bir xil menu, bir xil oynalar") → always the five tabs (a tab without permission shows "Bo'lim yopiq" inside instead of disappearing; Topshiriqlar shows "Topshiriq yo'q" without an HR row) and always the same cards on Bugun (lesson analysis, fines, polls, the dark "next lesson" card say "yo'q" when empty); header = "O'qituvchi · homeroom · two subjects +N" → before, tabs and cards came and went with data, and the homeroom was cut off for teachers with many subjects.
- [2026-10-07] "Chiqish" then another account in the Mini App → a Mini App session carries the Telegram it came from (`tg_uid` claim); unlink uses it (no longer fails on a 24 h old initData or a replaced panel key), returns a link ticket, and after logout the Telegram login screen (not the browser one) links the new account at once → before, a refused logout left the old link, and a keyboard-launched logout showed the browser login whose session was not linked.
- [2026-10-07] Removing a paid subscription month (client: "to'langan bo'lsa ham ... butunlay o'chirish, tranzaksiyalari arxiv sifatida qolsin, lekin hisob-kitobdan o'chirilsin"; asked: the money leaves the accounts too) → one transaction: every receipt that paid the month is reversed (storno row, mirror ledger — the original stays as archive), the part it paid for other months is re-issued as a new receipt with the same allocations, the month's adjustments and accrual batch are reversed, the invoice becomes void + removed_at; accrual never bills it again when the subscription is edited (only a subscription opened later does); optional "delete the subscription" when all its months go → payments, allocations and the ledger are append-only, so removal is built from reversals and the cash only loses the removed months' share.
- [2026-10-07] Two-person rule for removal → asked the client: money only — whoever took a receipt cannot remove the month it paid (the preview marks it); the month's accrual (background job / saved subscription, posted under some admin's name) does not count as own (`LedgerService.ReverseAccrualAsync`, invoice batches only) → the background accrual posts as the first superadmin, so the old rule blocked that account on most months.
- [2026-10-07] Attendance in reports counts PEOPLE (client: "7 soat dars bo'lsa va biror o'quvchi umuman kelmasa 7 ta odam kelmagan bo'lib chiqyapti ... bitta o'quvchi bir kishiga yurish kerak") → a student's held lessons of a date collapse into one day (`StudentDay`): absent in every checked lesson = one "kelmadi"; absent in some (asked: "kelgan bo'ladi") = present, counted apart as partial ("ayrim darslarga kelmadi"); late = present with any late mark; nothing checked = unchecked. Applied to: Kunlik davomat card, Davomat belgilash overview (and the MCP tools on it), attendance analytics totals/classes/trend/reasons, dashboard attendance rate and class chart. The period cut, a lesson's own list and per-student lesson details ("O'tilgan dars / Qatnashgan / Qoldirgan") stay per lesson — they are about lessons, and say so.
- [2026-10-07] Discipline points for attendance reasons → once per (student, date, reason) — asked: "kuniga bir marta" (absent all 7 lessons = one penalty, a late mark also once a day).
- [2026-10-07] Mini App linking → login + password only (client: "kod bilan ulash kerakmas ... hamma login parol bilan ulaydi"): the code form is gone from the Mini App and the server texts; the old code endpoint stays unused (no screen calls it) → no reason to keep a second way in.
- [2026-10-07] Login spelling → found regardless of case, spaces and apostrophes, and from the first two words of a full name, only when exactly one account matches (web and Mini App) → on the server 148 distinct Mini App sign-ins failed in a month, mostly "Familiya Ism" and capitalised first letters; the password is still required.
- [2026-10-07] Other Mini App sign-in gaps closed → the link ticket also travels in the body/header (Telegram Web runs the app in a cross-site iframe); a Telegram linked to an archived account is unlinked so it can sign in to another one; linking from the keyboard button returns the new button's key so an expired token re-signs-in by itself; "Chiqish" in the web panel opened from Telegram unlinks that Telegram too (only an explicit logout, never a token expiry).
- [2026-10-07] A class nobody took attendance for (client: "bitta sinf davomat qilinmagan lekin bu haqda malumot hisobi chiqmagan") → attendance analytics, the attendance-discipline report and the dashboard "by period" widget also take the TIMETABLE lessons up to today (Sundays and holidays excluded; on the dashboard only lessons already started): one nobody conducted or marked counts its pupils as "tekshirilmagan", never as a conducted lesson → before, such a class vanished from the totals (prod 07.10: 7-A, 26 pupils). The discipline report's attendance % is now over CHECKED days — an unchecked day is neither present nor absent (it used to count as present). Class rate on the dashboard keeps held lessons only.
- [2026-10-07] Which months a subscription can remove → from its start month until the next subscription of the same category starts; its end date does not cut the list → prod: a subscription closed on the 2nd (to end in September) had October accrued on the 1st; removing September and the subscription left October unreachable.
- [2026-10-07] Mini App student/parent home is the same for everyone (client: "o'quvchilar profillari ham hammada har xil chiqyapti") → fixed card order; the money card no longer jumps above the lessons when there is a debt (red colour says it); polls, certificates and "Nimalar to'lanmagan" stay with an empty state; one failed side request shows on its own card instead of blanking the tab. Parent wording kept: the people using student logins in the Mini App are the parents.
- [2026-10-07] Oshxona menu location (client: "oshxona menusini future menudan chiqarib sozlamalar -> umumiy sozlamalar menusi ichiga alohida menu sifatida") → a section "Oshxona menyusi" of the Umumiy sozlamalar hub, shown only to whoever holds `app` (the canteen API's key); `/admin/canteen` keeps working → moving it under `settings` would have locked out roles that manage the menu without settings rights.
- [2026-10-08] Telegram opening the web portal instead of the Mini App (client photos: the web layout inside Telegram on one phone, the Mini App on another, same account) → any page of the web app or the landing opened BY TELEGRAM (launch data `#tgWebApp…` in the hash) goes to `/tg/` with the same query and hash → the bot's buttons in code all point to `/tg/`; the other way in is outside the code (BotFather "Open"/main Mini App URL or an old keyboard), so the page fixes it itself. The BotFather URL should still be set to `/tg/`.
- [2026-10-08] Archived pupils (client: "to'lov qilish, chegirma yoki abonement biriktirish yoki sinfga qo'shish butkul taqiqlansin ... deyarli amallar bajarish mumkin bo'lmasligi kerak ... tizimga ham kirolmasligi") → refused with 409 `student_archived`: payment, abonement create/edit, discount create/attach/approve, sick discount and its cancel, withdrawal cancel, profile edit (it could put the pupil back into a class), account/password, status tag, guardians, location, contracts, certificates, discipline points, grades, journal; plan repricing skips them; announcements skip them. Open: reading, comments, restore, and the close-out of what exists (payment/refund storno, refund, ending an abonement or a discount, removing a month, voiding) → otherwise a pupil who left could never be settled. To take money from an archived debtor, restore first. Sign-in was already refused (login, Mini App, old tokens) — now covered by a test.
- [2026-10-08] Report by abonement (client: "qaysi abonement bo'yicha nechta student bor, qancha to'lovlar bo'lgan ... faqat kerakli joyga") → on Moliya → Abonement tranzaksiyalari: an "Abonement" filter and a "Ro'yxat | Abonementlar bo'yicha" switch (pupils, billed, discount, paid, left, debtors per abonement, same filters; a line opens the list of that abonement) → no new menu entry (the Moliya menu mirrors EduSchool). An invoice's abonement = the one of the pupil's subscription of that category covering the month; none → "Abonementsiz" (imported balances, months billed after a close). The grouped view leaves voided months out unless "Bekor qilingan" is chosen.
- [2026-10-08] Switching accounts in the Mini App (client: "bitta oila farzandlari uchun 2 xil login parol bilan kirsa ham boshqa farzandini akkauntiga o'tishi oson bo'lishi kerak ... o'qituvchi va ota-ona akkauntini ham almashtirish oson bo'lishi kerak") → a Telegram keeps a list of saved accounts (`telegram_saved_accounts`); each is added ONCE with its own login and password, then switched to with one tap, no password; the active one is still `telegram_accounts.user_id` (the bot's panel button opens it). "Chiqish" removes the current account and opens the next saved one; the last one unlinks as before. Archived accounts drop out of the list. Existing links are backfilled by the migration → no automatic siblings by parent phone (a sibling's data would open without its password) and no new parent logins to hand out.
- [2026-10-08] Brothers and sisters behind one pupil's login (client: "bittasiniki bilan kirsa tepada o'ngga chapga boshqa farzandiga ham o'tib ketishi mumkin edi") → a pupil's login sees its family: pupils sharing a guardian (`student_guardians`) or the same parents' phone (last 9 digits); a phone on more than 6 pupils is a placeholder, not a family. The Mini App's switcher at the top shows them; every read and the family's pickup/polls/feedback honour `?studentId=` for a family member, a foreign id is ignored. Writes in the pupil's own name (chat, assignments, uploads) stay the pupil's own → parents have no logins of their own (0 parent accounts, 746 guardian records without accounts); before 2026-09-25 parents signed in with their phone and saw all children — this brings that back without new logins. The saved-accounts switcher stays for a teacher who is also a parent.
- [2026-10-08] A pupil's password in the CRM (client: "o'quvchi akkauntida parol yana ko'rinmaydigan bo'lib qolibdi") → signing in no longer wipes a PUPIL's visible initial password (it did on every login since 2026-10-06, 115 pupils on prod had lost it); it goes only when the pupil sets their own. Staff and teachers: unchanged. Passwords already wiped cannot be recovered (only the hash exists) — a reset in the profile shows a new one.
- [2026-10-08] Phone sharing removed from the bot (client: "telefon raqam ulashish ... kerakmas, botdan buni butunlay olib tashla"; "ota-onalar login parol bilan ... kiryapti ... shunda ularga boraversin") → no phone button anywhere; a shared contact is not stored. School notifications (all notifiers read `telegram_registrations`) now follow the accounts signed in on a chat: a pupil's login registers the pupil and the family, a teacher's the teacher; rows from the old phone sharing (they carry the phone) stay untouched; login-made rows go when the account leaves. Synced on every sign-in/switch/sign-out and for every linked chat at bot start → on prod 139 of 157 linked chats had received nothing because they never shared a phone.
- [2026-10-08] "Akkaunt qo'shish" in the parent/pupil profile (client: "endi akkaunt qo'shish degan joyi kerakmas") → gone there: the family is in the switcher at the top. The accounts list still shows in that profile when the Telegram holds several accounts — otherwise a teacher who switched into a child's account could not get back. Adding stays behind the teacher's button.
- [2026-10-08] Checked end to end (local, prod code): teacher → add child's login → parent panel with the 3 children → sibling → profile accounts list without "Akkaunt qo'shish" → back to the teacher without password → the bot's button opens the last chosen account → "Chiqish" from the child returns to the teacher; notifications: teacher + 3 children, then teacher only. Fixed on the way: the sign-out text now says the next saved account opens; a pupil's login no longer asks the parent-only news first (a 403 on every open).
- [2026-10-08] Bosh sahifa period filter — which preset is selected by default, and what does the filter move? → "Bugun" is the default (no URL params); `Bu hafta` starts on Monday; a custom range is capped at 12 calendar months and cannot reach past today. Dated figures follow the period (average grade, attendance rate, class chart, top classes, "Davomat analitikasi" summed per day, absentees, first payments in the hint, income/expense cards); monthly charts show at least the 6 months up to the period's end; month-based widgets (Moliyaviy holat, Abonement qamrovi, Bonus/Jarima) use the period's months; the schedule widget shows the period's last day. Headcounts, balances, debt state, cash balance, contingent and the lead funnel stay "now" → the buttons the client listed start with "Bugun" and the page was already a daily monitor; the 12-month cap is the finance dashboard's own limit, so no widget errors out; a "now" figure has no honest past value to filter.
- [2026-10-08] P&L showed "Yillik xarajat 0 so'm" although 169 EduSchool outflows (832 mln) were in the cash box (client: "eduschool bilan moliya bo'limlari funksionalliklari bir xil ishlashi kerak") → a TYPED cash-box Kirim/Chiqim now posts one ledger batch (`ref_type = cash_box_transaction`): Chiqim debit `expense:*` / credit cash|bank, Kirim debit cash|bank / credit `revenue:*`; cancelling it reverses the batch (no two-person check — the cash-box cancel has none); transfers and exchanges stay off the ledger → P&L, Moliya hisobotlari, Pul oqimi and Kassa kuni all read only the ledger, and EduSchool's P&L counts typed cash-box outflows.
- [2026-10-08] Which P&L row does a transaction type land in? → each type gets `transaction_types.ledger_account`, picked from the closed list (Chiqim: salary, rent, utilities, supplies, food, transport, dormitory, marketing, taxes, repair, other; Kirim: the revenue accounts); a new type without a choice is guessed from its name; existing types are filled from the name at start-up; changing it affects later rows only → per-type free-text accounts would break the closed-list rule (existing-module-gaps §3.4); five accounts (food, transport, dormitory, marketing, taxes) were added because otherwise ~190 mln of EduSchool outflows sat in "Boshqa chiqim".
- [2026-10-08] Untyped cash-box outflows → stay off the ledger → on prod they are EduSchool's terminal/Click → bank moves ("хисоб ракамга тушди", "утказилди", "айрибошлаш", 164.6 mln), not spending; the Chiqim form already requires a type, so new untyped outflows cannot appear. The client agreed to this plan.
- [2026-10-08] Existing rows → `CashBoxLedgerSync.RunAsync` at start-up posts the missing batch of every typed, not-cancelled Kirim/Chiqim with the row's own date and author; it is idempotent; cancelled rows are skipped (their net is zero) → a one-off SQL would duplicate the posting rule; the code path is the same as live posting and is covered by tests.
- [2026-10-09] EduSchool vs LMS for this school year (from 02.09.2026) → inflows (484 pupil payments + 5 exchange halves), 168 outflows and 26 transfers match 1:1; the only money not in LMS was 21 September payments for 2025–2026 debts (23 515 000), entered in EduSchool under the old year. Client: "eng maqbul yo'lni qil" → written to prod as cash-box Kirim in "Ulhanoy Karimova" on their actual dates with a new Kirim type "O'tgan yil qarzi to'lovi" (`revenue:tuition`), pupil linked, EduSchool № in the note; plus 4 transfers (14 540 000) to "Yusupov Mirzoulug'bek" on 24.09 — the part of EduSchool's old-year balance move that came from these receipts → as pupil payments they would have shown a false advance (LMS holds no old-year debt), and without the transfers Ulhanoy's box would be overstated. Ids are uuid5, the SQL refuses a second run; backup `/root/wunderkind_2026-10-08_2020_eski-qarz-oldidan.dump`.
- [2026-10-09] Salary paid from the cash box did not reach the salary reports (September: 360 mln as "Hodimga oylik / avans" Chiqim rows; reports read only `expenses`) → `cash_box_transactions` gets nullable `teacher_id` / `employee_user_id` (at most one, pay_out only, FK RESTRICT); only a type whose account is `expense:salary` may carry one; the Chiqim form asks for "Xodim" when such a type is chosen (list from `GET /api/admin/cash-boxes/salary-recipients`, under the cash-box permission); `SalaryPaymentQuery` adds these not-cancelled rows to the `expenses` ones → the query is the single source for the five salary screens, so all of them follow; parsing the name out of the note at read time would be a guess.
- [2026-10-09] Existing September payouts → linked on prod by name from the note (one SQL, run by the client): 104 of 121 rows (329.4 mln) matched a teacher (89) or a staff user (15), an archived duplicate loses to the live record, spelling variants x/h and -xon/-bek were accepted (5 names, listed in the session); 17 rows (31.06 mln) of 10 people who are not in LMS at all stay unlinked → they appear in P&L "Maosh" but under nobody in the salary report until those people are added.
- [2026-10-09] "Tranzaksiyalar" did not list cash-box operations (EduSchool's does) → third source in `TransactionJournalQuery`: `cash_in`, `cash_out`, `cash_transfer`, `cash_exchange`; a cancel is its own row with the opposite sign (like a payment storno); transfers and exchanges have direction `internal`, are listed and never counted in Σ kirim / Σ chiqim; category filter matches the type's P&L account; receipt-number and first-payment filters exclude them → the page promises every money movement in one list.
- [2026-10-09] `MiniAppParentPortalTests.Receipt_to_telegram…` failed in every full run with 429 → the test now sends from its own X-Forwarded-For address like the other rate-limited tests; the limit itself (20/min per IP on `/api/tg/*`) is unchanged → the assertion was right, the shared TestServer address was the problem.
- [2026-10-09] "Davomat analitikasi" names the students (client: "qaysi o'quvchilar qaysi kuni qaysi sababdan kelmagan ... bilish mumkin bo'lsin") → the same report adds a row per student over the period and a row per student-day that was not a plain "came" (absent / partial / late / unchecked) with its reasons and lesson numbers; capped at 5000 newest rows with a "narrow the range" note → the page already counts student-days, so the list is the breakdown of its own cards, not a new definition.
- [2026-10-09] "Cardlarni bosganda card o'zining hisoboti bo'yicha kerakli joyga olib o'tsin" → in-page: O'quvchilar/Keldi → the students table (or the month grid when a class is chosen), Kelmadi/Tekshirilmagan/Kech keldi → the list pre-filtered by that status; reason chips filter the list by reason; a class row selects the class; a student row or name filters the list to that student → each card's report is on this page; leaving the page would lose the chosen class and period.
- [2026-10-09] Month grid (client sent the grades-report table as the model; "hozircha soatlar emas kun bo'yicha olsa yetadi") → with one class chosen, pupils × days of the period: ✓ came, reason short code for a full-day absence (red when unexcused), ½ missed some lessons, KQ late, ? unchecked, blank — no lessons; lesson numbers only in the cell tooltip; "Shu oy" quick range added → the server sends each pupil's lesson days for one class only, so "no event" can safely mean "came".
- [2026-10-09] Abonementlar analitikasi: which pupils and which money count in a period? → an abonement counts when its term meets the period; money is the invoices of the months the period touches (billed = amount − discount, paid = effective allocations), each tied to the abonement covering its month; pupils who left appear in an "Arxiv" row only when the period has something of theirs → the same invoice/plan rule as "Abonement tranzaksiyalari", so the two screens agree.
- [2026-10-09] Abonementlar analitikasi presets → "Bu oy", "O'tgan oy", "O'quv yili" take whole months (not "up to today") → invoices are monthly; a pupil starting mid-month would otherwise be in the money but not in the head-count. Custom range capped at 400 days.
- [2026-10-09] Menu placement → first entry of Moliya / AMALLAR, despite the "do not add entries" note on that list → the client asked for exactly that place.
- [2026-10-09] Sinflar jadvali: "Oylik to'lov" column removed from the screen only; `classes.monthly_fee`, the form field and the Excel export are untouched → the client asked only about the table column.
- [2026-10-09] "Bitta o'qituvchi bir nechta sinfga sinf rahbari" → new `class_homeroom_teachers` (class_id, teacher_id) is the source of truth, backfilled from `teachers.homeroom_class` by class name (the active class when an archived one shares the name); the old column stays in the database but the app no longer reads it; every reader (teacher app homeroom list, pick-ups, handover/accept checks, journal and class pages, student card, reports, search, MCP, chat channels, year archive) now takes all of a teacher's classes; responses still carry `homeroomClass`, now the joined label "5-A, 6-B, Aniq fanlar" → one teacher row could not hold several classes, and assigning a second class used to take the first away.
- [2026-10-09] Track groups' homeroom teacher → the group's own teacher (`study_group_teachers`, already labelled "Sinf rahbari" in the group form since 2026-09-29), shown in the classes table and in the teacher's label; no second table for groups → the client already sets it there.
- [2026-10-09] Teacher form: "Sinf rahbarligi" became a multi-pick of classes, sent as `homeroomClassIds`; a save without the list (old client, list not loaded yet) leaves homeroom untouched → never wipe assignments by accident.
- [2026-10-09] Abonementlar analitikasi cards and abonement rows are filters → each opens the whole school's pupils it counts (one server rule for the number and the list), with Excel; "Abonement ko'rsatilmagan" now counts the pupils billed with no covering abonement (it showed 0 pupils with money) → the client asked to find exactly those pupils through the cards.
- [2026-10-09] Excel for the analytics → page button: workbook with "Sinflar", "Abonement turlari", "O'quvchilar" (in school + those who left but have something in the period); class page and card lists export their own rows; money cells are real numbers.
- [2026-10-11] Change list item 4 (groups) → not done: the client fixes group membership themselves ("uni biz qilamiz").
- [2026-10-11] Item 11 (message after payment) → already in place: every accepted payment sends the PDF receipt to the parent's Telegram; it fails only for parents not connected (259/480 students connected on prod; 7 receipts skipped in a week) → no new code; the new students-list "Telegram: ulanmagan" filter finds those parents.
- [2026-10-11] Item 16 → the two dashboard widgets moved to Davomat analitikasi and were removed from the dashboard; the dashboard API still computes their fields (unused now) → keeps older clients and tests working; can be dropped later.
- [2026-10-11] Item 13 push → removed completely: FcmService, device tokens, push history, Firebase settings screen, push endpoints, teacher-PWA registration and the firebase dependency; tables `device_tokens`, `push_messages` and the `school_meta.fcm_*` columns are dropped (client approved; deploy takes a DB dump first). Pick-up "parent arrived" → Telegram to the homeroom teacher(s); "handed over"/"accepted" → Telegram to the parent. Journal grade/absence pushes are not replaced (a message per grade would spam); absences reach parents through item 10.
- [2026-10-11] Item 10 → button "Kelmaganlarga xabar" on Davomat belgilash: only pupils absent the WHOLE day (the screen's "N o'quvchi kelmadi" rule), message with the reason, at most one per pupil per day (`absence_notices`, unique student+date), a preview shows who gets it and who has no Telegram; sending needs the `attendance` permission.
- [2026-10-11] Item 9 (announcement details, "kimga") → every announcement now stores its recipients (`broadcast_recipients`: pupil, parent chat, sent / failed / no_telegram — pupils without Telegram included); clicking a sent announcement opens who got it, who did not and who has no Telegram. Announcements sent before 2026-10-11 show only the old totals. The parent Mini App now resolves announcements by these rows, so "selected"/"filter"/"group" announcements reach the right child's feed (old ones keep the label rule).
- [2026-10-11] Item 12 (bold, italic …) → Telegram-client style marks in the text box (**qalin**, __kursiv__, ++tagiga++, ~~chizilgan~~, ||spoiler||, `kod`, ```blok```, [matn](https://…), "> " quote) with a toolbar and a live preview, instead of raw HTML tags or a WYSIWYG editor (fragile, and the marks are what Telegram users already type). Stored text is what was typed; `TelegramHtml.FromMarkup` escapes everything first and only then turns the marks into Telegram HTML, so the result is safe for Telegram and for innerHTML. Used in announcements (main panel, roster and students-list message windows) and news (Telegram message + portal and Mini App feeds via a sanitized `bodyHtml`). A message Telegram cannot parse is re-sent once as plain text; other rejections are not retried.
- [2026-10-11] Item 15 → evening-lesson and dorm roll-calls use hand-kept rosters (`boarding_rosters`, one per session, dated: added on / taken off), not dormitory subscriptions; only roster pupils are listed and can be marked; "Ro'yxat" window to add (search) and take off (from today; a row added today is deleted, past marks stay). Migration puts today's dormitory subscribers on both rosters and keeps earlier days' history of former boarders, so nothing disappears at cut-over.
- [2026-10-11] Item 14 ("kunduzgi davomat faqat hamshirada, kechki yotoq komendantlarda") → marking daytime attendance is its own permission `attendanceMark` (viewing stays `attendance`); the marking endpoints moved to DailyAttendanceController (same URLs). Data on deploy: the "Hamshira" role and its users get `attendanceMark` and lose evening/dorm; a "Komendant" role (evening + dorm) is created — no commandant accounts exist on prod yet, the admin assigns it when they are added. The three managers (two deputies, academic director) keep viewing daytime/evening/dorm but can no longer mark daytime attendance.
- [2026-10-11] Item 17 → new page "Davomat analitikasi 2.0" (Davomat menu, `attendance` perm): three cards (daytime from the existing analytics, evening, dorm) and, per evening/dorm session, stat cards, a day chart, groups, reasons, pupils (most absent first) and "who, which day, why", with Excel. Unit — pupil-day of the session roster; "unmarked" counts only on days a roll-call was taken (at least one mark), so weekends do not show the whole roster as unmarked. Sessions the user cannot read are hidden.
- [2026-10-11] Item 6 "kirish-chiqish vaqtlari, istalgancha tahrirlash, sinflarda har xil" → the existing lesson_times stays the MAIN schedule; any number of extra bell schedules (bell_schedules/_periods/_owners), each class or group (lesson owner) assigned to at most one; every reader of lesson times (portals, Mini App, teacher app, daily attendance, substitutions, turnstile expectations, dashboard, MCP) resolves the time by the lesson's owner through BellTimes → keeps today's data untouched and lets primary/secondary grades differ.
- [2026-10-11] Teacher clash checks with mixed bell schedules → the master grid and the lesson-slot modal compare real time overlap when both lessons have times (period number otherwise); the server-side pupil conflict check (G-11) stays period-based → period numbers still identify a slot inside one owner, and class/group lessons of the same pupils normally share one schedule.
- [2026-10-11] Turnstile "by period" grouping and attendance analytics by period keep the main schedule's labels → they aggregate by period number across all classes; per-owner times only change expected arrival/leave, which now use each class's own first/last lesson clock.
- [2026-10-11] Item 7 "Dars jadvaliga o'zgartirish kiritish — muharrir noqulay" → the real pain was that the ACTIVE schedule was locked in "Dars jadvali yaratish": one lesson change meant copying every class into a new draft (~900 requests) and re-publishing. Now the active schedule is edited in place from the same grid: a click opens the usual cell window, a drag moves a lesson to another period of the same row (or swaps two), both saved atomically (`live-cells`). History stays intact by copy-on-write: if the class's template is also assigned to weeks before this calendar week, the server copies it, moves this and later weeks to the copy (same name) and renames the old one "… · dd.MM.yyyy gacha" for the past weeks; an old version used only in past weeks cannot be edited. The per-class editor uses the same path; drafts are created with one request per class; old versions are ignored by teacher-busy checks.
- [2026-10-11] Live schedule changes take effect from the CURRENT week (whole week, like "Nashr qilish"), not from tomorrow → week assignments are per week; splitting a week would need a new model. Planned future changes still go through a draft + publish.
- [2026-10-11] Client current-week bug fixed on the way → on Sundays the browser resolved the current week to week 1 (the server already handled it), so "Nashr qilish" on a Sunday would have re-assigned the whole quarter including past weeks; now Sunday belongs to the week that started on Monday.
- [2026-10-11] Review fixes for items 6–7 → (a) salary/turnstile/MCP "main template": while an owner has a template assigned to this or a later week, its past-only versions are left out (otherwise the archived copy won by lesson count); otherwise the old "most lessons" rule is unchanged (pinned by TeacherSalaryCalcTests). (b) On Sundays the edit/publish boundary is next Monday — the week that just ended is history. (c) Templates used in past weeks (incl. "… gacha" versions) cannot be deleted (409; delete hidden for old versions). (d) Rows that are not on the active schedule show "faol emas" with a one-class "Faollashtirish". (e) Drag & drop warns about a teacher clash first; cells are role=button divs so Firefox can drag. (f) Deleting/graduating a class removes its bell-schedule assignment; stale ids no longer block saving. (g) Bell schedules are capped at 10 periods, like every schedule editor. (h) Concurrent live edits lock the template row.
- [2026-10-11] Year rollover keeps a promoted class on its bell schedule (class id is unchanged, 4-A → 5-A) → the school re-assigns bell schedules at the start of the year; we do not guess grade bands.
- [2026-10-11] "Umumiy chat ota-onalar, o'qituvchilar akkauntlarida ham ko'rinmasligi kerak" → the group chat now exists only in the admin panel (Future → Guruh chati). Removed: the teacher PWA "Suhbat" tab, the Mini App teacher "Xabarlar" tab and its unread counter, the teacher entry in the admin-client nav, the teacher "Xabarlar (chat)" permission toggle, and the server routes /api/teacher/chat/*, /api/student/chat*, /api/tg/teacher/chat/* (404 now); the SignalR hub no longer joins teachers, pupils or parents to any channel. Chat history stays in the database for the admin.
- [2026-10-11] Found on the way: the parent Mini App "Maktab e'lonlari" card was reading the class GROUP CHAT, not announcements → it now reads real announcements for the child (/api/student/announcements, same rule as the parent Telegram endpoint: recipient rows, older ones by class label), as plain text.
- [2026-10-11] Post-deploy check of item 14 on prod: roles also carry PAGE grants ("/admin/…|edit"), which open menu pages and re-emit section keys when a role is re-saved → migration AttendancePageGrants removes the evening/dorm pages from "Hamshira" (role and its users) and the "Davomat belgilash" page from every role/user without `attendanceMark` (e.g. Akademik Director keeps viewing attendance but no longer sees the marking page).
- [2026-10-12] Superadmins in Boshqaruv → Xodimlar? → listed with a "Tizim egasi" badge; only a superadmin opens/edits their row (credentials, password), never deleted or given a role/permissions → client: "super admin bo'lsa ham umumiy xodimlar ro'yxatida bo'lishi kerak"; a "staff" grant must not be able to reset an owner's password.
- [2026-10-12] Teacher who is also a dorm commandant ("o'qituvchi profilida role berish mumkin bo'lsin") → the superadmin gives the TEACHER account an access role (teacher form → "Qo'shimcha rol", the same Boshqaruv → Rollar roles; PUT /api/admin/teachers/{id}/role). Only roles whose section keys are all evening/dorm roll-call keys are offered and accepted (page grants ignored), and only those keys ever reach a teacher's request even if the role is widened later. The Mini App shows a "Komendant" card on "Bugun" (no new tab — the bottom menu stays the same for every teacher) that opens today's roll call with the admin page's rules (unmarked by default, Joyida/Yo'q, reason under Yo'q, Sababsiz → parent Telegram, everyone marked before saving). Roster edits and statistics stay in the admin panel; the old teacher PWA (ui-web) is unchanged.
- [2026-10-12] Teachers want to give the lesson topic and homework from the Mini App ("baho qo'yish, vazifa berish, mavzu berish") → the lesson screen (Jurnal / "Davomat va baho") gets "Dars mavzusi" and "Uyga vazifa" fields saved with the lesson note on "Saqlash" (the existing PUT /api/teacher/journal/notes; no backend change); the parent/student Mini App "Jadval" shows "Mavzu:" next to "Uy vazifasi:". No Telegram message to parents for homework (not asked); the full LMS assignments module (test/file/video with submissions) stays in the old teacher PWA.
- [2026-10-12] Who may assign access roles ("rolelarga dostupi bor odam ham role biriktira olsin") → superadmin, admin, and a staff member with the Rollar page at the full level (page grant `/admin/boshqaruv/roles|edit`, or a legacy role with the full `staff` key); never their own role, never a role or a member with section keys they do not hold themselves (`RoleAssignment`), owners untouched; creating/editing roles and hand-picked permissions stay superadmin-only → opening assignment without the subset rule would let a role assigner hand finance or any other section to a colleague or a second account.
- [2026-10-12] Where do pupils' assignments live in the Mini App ("mini appdagi topshiriqlar qism bu xodimlarniki, men o'quvchilar uchun alohidani nazarda tutyapman")? → teacher: a fifth bottom tab "Vazifalar" (list, new/edit — test, written, file, video — results and grading), HR "Topshiriqlar" unchanged; pupil/parent: a "Vazifalar" card on Bosh opening a full-screen list and detail → same `/api/teacher|student/assignments` the old web app uses, no backend change; the parent tab bar stays at five.
- [2026-10-12] Assignment rules the server does not enforce → the Mini App enforces them for pupils: a test is submitted once (no retake to raise the score), after the due date only if the teacher allowed late work; written/file/video may be resubmitted until then. The start date and late penalty are not asked in the Mini App form (the server ignores both; an edit keeps the stored values). Only http(s) and site paths become links (a pupil's `javascript:` link is shown as text).
- [2026-10-12] The bottom tab bar came back the instant focus left a text field, so the first tap after typing (on a chip or radio near the bottom) landed on the bar and was lost → `useKeyboardOpen` now re-checks 250 ms after the field loses focus (a text field still hides the bar at once).
- [2026-10-12] Who confirms a cash-box transfer ("Kassir menga jo'natilgan pullar o'zi tasdiqlanib qolyapti")? → **the person responsible for the receiving box, and only them** — not the sender, not the owner/superadmin; a box with no responsible person, or a transfer the receiver sent themselves, takes effect at once → the point is that whoever holds the money confirms it arrived; the cashier currently signs in as the "Tizim administratori" superadmin, so an owner override would let her confirm her own handover. If the box changes hands, the new responsible person answers.
- [2026-10-12] When does an accepted transfer move the money? → **as of the moment it was sent** (its `created_at`); until then it counts in neither box, and a returned one never counts → the handover happens when the cashier sends it; the answer only confirms it, usually the same evening. Daily statements stay on the day the money physically changed hands.
- [2026-10-12] Can a pending transfer be cancelled (storno)? → **no — the receiver returns it; a returned one has nothing to cancel** (409 `transfer_pending` / `transfer_returned`); an accepted one is cancelled the usual way → one way to undo each state, and no storno row for money that never moved. Transfers written before 2026-10-12 stay as they were (`needs_acceptance = false`).
- [2026-10-12] Cash-box journal export → **a server-built .xlsx of exactly the rows on screen** (the client posts their ids; POST because a day's ids do not fit a URL, allowed for view-only finance users) instead of the browser CSV → the CSV opened in Excel as one quoted column with garbled letters, showed 05:00 on every row (the date-only field) and put the pupil in "Kassir". Other `exportToCsv` screens are unchanged.
- [2026-10-12] "Guruhlarni ichidan ham xuddi sinflar kabi ro'yxatni yuklab olish" — the classes list has an "Excel" button, a class's roster has none → **both for groups**: "Excel" on the groups list (same filters as the screen: search, grades, subject, archive) and on a group's roster (its pupils; with "Tarix bilan" on, leavers with the date and reason too); server-built .xlsx with a title line → covers both readings of "ichidan" (the Groups section, or inside a group). The class roster was not given one unasked.
- [2026-10-12] Homeroom teachers and pupils' money ("sinf rahbar ... rahbarligidagi guruh yoki sinfini ko'radimi asosan qarzdorliklar uchun") → **a narrow exception to "teachers never see money" (P1-21)**: only the pupils of the classes a teacher leads and of the track groups they teach (HomeroomDirectory's rule), read-only — debt, unpaid months, days overdue and the parent's phone (tap to call, for reminders); no payments or discounts. One endpoint `GET /api/teacher/homeroom/debts` for both the Mini App ("Sinfim" card on Bugun + full list) and the web portal (debt line on Sinf rahbarligi). The number is the admin "Qarzdorlar" report's own (`DebtorsAsync`, archived pupils excluded) → teacher and accountant never see two amounts for one pupil. The parent phone was not explicitly asked about; included because a reminder needs it and homeroom teachers already contact parents.

Who a chat is: parent — bot registration name + pupil, or Mini App parent account + guardian's children; employee — teacher registration or a staff-role account, with the role; student — pupil account. "Not connected": active pupils whose parents have no chat, teachers and staff/admin accounts with none (pupils' own accounts are optional, so not listed). News sent before 2026-10-12 keep only the counts — the list cannot be rebuilt.

The web portal button exports all debtors (its list is the pickup screen).

- [2026-10-13] Automatic messages to parents (absence, new monthly invoice, debt reminder, birthday) → **one rules module, every rule shipped OFF**; the school approves each text and switches it on (Xabarlar → Avtomatik xabarlar). Parent chats = bot registrations, as for broadcasts; a line whose token is empty (no absence reason) is dropped.

- [2026-10-15] Substitute pay ("o'rindosh ustozning oylik summasi") → **kept as built on 2026-10-07: +1 lesson at the substitute's own rate, −1 for the absent teacher; now shown next to allowances, bonuses and fines in the detail and the ledger** → it was already automatic; nothing in the request asks for a separate fixed amount per substituted lesson.

- [2026-10-15] Monthly test top/bottom messages — which exam is "monthly", how ties count, where it goes → **any exam with pupils (the type name is the school's); rank by percent then points, equal results share a place and all on a place within the count are included; bottom never repeats a top pupil; whole exam or per class; to every Telegram chat linked to the pupil + a Broadcast row so it stays in the pupil's Mini App feed; once per exam, pupil and kind** → "top 3" cutting a tie arbitrarily would be unfair to the family left out; the pupil's "account" in this system is the family's linked chats and the app feed.

- [2026-10-15] Range board limits and compatibility → **from ≤ to, at most 93 days (400 in Uzbek otherwise); `?month=` and no parameters still work; an unparseable month is now 400 instead of a silently empty board; rows = active teachers with lessons in the range plus anyone with an entry in it (archived never shown, as before)** → a quarter fits; nothing recorded disappears from view.

- [2026-10-15] "Kelmadi" when the pupil was absent but nobody marked homework → shown as `Y` (muted, `derived`) when the cell's attendance reason is not a lateness reason (an unknown/deleted reason counts as absence, like `SlotFullyGradedAsync`); never written; counted in the Y column → the rule from the task; a lateness reason means the pupil was in class.

- [2026-10-15] Base 100 and attendance-reason points → **kept as they are (100 + manual + attendance-reason points once per pupil, day and reason); the profile sidebar now shows that score instead of the raw sum** → the client's existing rule (EduSchool starts at 0; ours is friendlier and already in use); the sidebar showed a different number from the overview — a bug.
- [2026-10-15] Behaviour on the Mini App lesson screen → **a "Xulq" chip on every pupil row (current score + a badge of today's points, all authors), a bottom sheet opening on "Salbiy", reason chips with points, optional note, "Qo'yish" saves at once and closes; the last 20 entries with delete on own entries of today; if the reasons call fails the chip is hidden (the lesson still works)** → most entries are violations; one tap to open, two to record; the lesson's "Saqlash" stays only for grades/homework.

- [2026-10-15] What is a "missed day" for the red list? → **the attendance report's own pupil-day (`StudentDay`): absent the whole day with an unexcused main reason — the same "sababsiz kun" the absentees card counts; a partly-present day ends a run; a day nobody checked or without lessons is neutral; an excused full day ends a run (counted only when `count_excused` is on)** → one definition on every attendance screen (the scan calls `AttendanceAnalytics.BuildAsync` through a new optional per-day callback); an explained day is not part of an unexplained run.
- [2026-10-15] Red-list criteria → **3 consecutive school days, or 5 missed days within 14 calendar days (today included); a consecutive run opens a case only if it ended inside that window; all editable on the page** → the client's "3+, 5+ ketma-ket"; on the first deploy a run the pupil recovered from weeks ago must not open a case.

- [2026-10-15] After "Hal bo'ldi" → **only days after the closed case's last missed day count for the pupil; a new run opens a new case; a closed case takes no more actions (409)** → resolving must not reopen it on the next scan; "a later new streak opens a new case".
- [2026-10-15] Case numbers while open → **`streak` = the longest run seen while open (never lowered), `window_count` and last absent / last present = as of the last scan, "Qaytdi" when last present > last absent** → the teacher sees what triggered the case even after the pupil came back.

- [2026-10-15] Who is told, and how → **Telegram only, staff only, both switches ON: (1) homeroom teachers of the class + the pupil's track-group teachers, one message per teacher per scan listing their new cases; (2) a daily digest at the 13:00 scan to staff accounts holding `attendanceCases` with a linked Mini App account, only when overdue cases exist (admin / superadmin accounts without the key get none; the page highlights anyway); no parent is ever messaged; texts neutral until approved** → the request opens the task "for the homeroom teacher and management"; the key is what makes someone a follow-up person; staff messages ship on like fine and substitution notices.
- [2026-10-15] Scan schedule → **fixed 13:00 and 18:00, school days only (not Sunday, not a holiday), each slot once; plus "Hozir tekshirish"** → nothing new to read on days off; keeps the settings card small.
- [2026-10-15] Red-list permissions → **admin API reads follow the AdminPerm rule (any staff, as `/api/admin/attendance`); actions, criteria and scan need the new `attendanceCases` key; the page sits under Davomat on `attendance` and its "To'liq" grant also emits `attendanceCases` (PAGE_EXTRA_SECTIONS); deleting an action is admin-side only** → "view = attendance, work = new key"; legacy roles with only `attendance` see the page and get the usual read-only toast on actions.
- [2026-10-15] Teacher scope for the red list → **classes the teacher leads + track groups they teach (the homeroom-debts circle); another pupil's case answers 404** → nothing about another class leaks; same circle the teacher already sees money for.

- [2026-10-15] Call-back day → **optional, tomorrow when left out, today..+60 days, kept only with "Qayta qo'ng'iroq"** → one tap fewer in the Mini App; a date is what makes the overdue rule work.
- [2026-10-15] Red-list dashboard widget and PWA → **no dashboard widget (the widget system needs access plumbing in four shared files; page + Mini App card cover the request); teacher PWA gets a read-only banner and a line per pupil, acting stays in the Mini App** → Mini App is the teachers' main surface; the PWA build cannot be run here (no node_modules volume), so the change there is minimal.

- [2026-10-15] Auto-message statuses: where do "Rejalashtirilgan / Tasdiq kutmoqda / … / To'xtatilgan" live? → **a new `auto_message_runs` table (one per rule and period: status scheduled|pending_approval|sending|done|cancelled, planned time, counts, approver/canceller) plus the same statuses on `auto_message_log` rows (scheduled, pending_approval, cancelled added), with `run_id`, `attempts`, `error`, `planned_at`, `ref_id`, `source`, `sent_by`** → the client wants both the run and the per-chat delivery list visible for audit; one row per chat already existed.
- [2026-10-15] "Rejalashtirilgan" — computed for display or stored? → **stored: the scheduler's first tick of the day creates each enabled rule's run as `scheduled` with its planned time (monthly rules from their day through the 2-day window); nothing is planned while the bot is not configured** → a stored run can be stopped before it sends and keeps its history; a computed one could not be cancelled.

- [2026-10-15] Stopping a run that is sending → **cancel = one conditional UPDATE of the run and of its waiting rows; the sender re-reads the run's status before each pupil; a delivered row keeps `sent`; a cancelled run is final for its period (switching the rule on again the same day does not reopen it)** → no lost update between the two requests, and the log never says "stopped" for a message that went out.
- [2026-10-15] Retrying failed messages → **a failed row of a run is re-sent on later 2-minute ticks while its period is current, up to 3 attempts in total; no extra backoff** → fixes the old bug (the period closed with failures and they were never retried); the tick interval is the backoff, and a blocked bot stays failed after 3 tries instead of hammering Telegram.
- [2026-10-15] Absence cut-off ("11:30 gacha") → **nullable `cutoff_time` (default = send time); only lessons whose bell start is before it count; a period with no start time is ignored; with no bell schedule at all every lesson counts; a class/track group with an unmarked pre-cut-off lesson is skipped and named in the run's note** → a later lesson must not turn an absent pupil into "partial", and half-taken attendance must not tell a parent their child did not come.
- [2026-10-15] Which pupils does an unmarked lesson affect? → **pupils of that class (whole-class lesson or their own half) and active members of that group** → mirrors who sits in the lesson; skipping too many is safe (no wrong message), skipping too few is not.

- [2026-10-15] Payment log rows → **one row per chat per send (automatic and the cashier's resend), `period` = the school day of the send, `ref_id` = receipt number, outside the once-per-period unique indexes; skipped when the pupil row does not exist; never affects the send** → a receipt may legitimately be sent several times, and each send is an audit fact.
